def mark_delinquent_C2() -> None:

def assess_late_fee_C2(ws_late_payment_fee: Decimal, ws_total_fees: Decimal) -> Decimal:

def calculate_premiums_C2(insurance_master: list[dict[str, Any]], ins_life: bool, ins_health: bool, ins_auto: bool, ins_home: bool, ins_umbrella: bool, ins_coverage_amount: Decimal, ws_life_rate_per_1000: Decimal, ws_health_base_premium: Decimal, ws_auto_base_premium: Decimal, ws_home_rate_per_1000: Decimal, ws_umbrella_rate: Decimal, ins_claims_count: int, ws_total_premiums: Decimal) -> None:
    """Calculate premiums."""
    print("CALCULATING PREMIUMS...")
    ws_not_eof = True
    ws_eof = False
    while not ws_eof:
        for insurance_record in insurance_master:
            if not insurance_record:
                ws_eof = True
            else:
                determine_base_premium_C2(insurance_record, ins_life, ins_health, ins_auto, ins_home, ins_umbrella, ins_coverage_amount, ws_life_rate_per_1000, ws_health_base_premium, ws_auto_base_premium, ws_home_rate_per_1000, ws_umbrella_rate)
                apply_risk_factor_C2(insurance_record, ins_claims_count)
                calculate_final_premium_C2(insurance_record, ws_total_premiums)

def determine_base_premium_C2(insurance_record: dict[str, Any], ins_life: bool, ins_health: bool, ins_auto: bool, ins_home: bool, ins_umbrella: bool, ins_coverage_amount: Decimal, ws_life_rate_per_1000: Decimal, ws_health_base_premium: Decimal, ws_auto_base_premium: Decimal, ws_home_rate_per_1000: Decimal, ws_umbrella_rate: Decimal) -> None:
    """Determine base premium."""
    global WS_CALC_AMOUNT
    if insurance_record["type"] == "life":
        WS_CALC_AMOUNT = ins_coverage_amount / 1000 * ws_life_rate_per_1000
    elif insurance_record["type"] == "health":
        WS_CALC_AMOUNT = ws_health_base_premium
    elif insurance_record["type"] == "auto":
        WS_CALC_AMOUNT = ws_auto_base_premium
    elif insurance_record["type"] == "home":
        WS_CALC_AMOUNT = ins_coverage_amount / 1000 * ws_home_rate_per_1000
    elif insurance_record["type"] == "umbrella":
        WS_CALC_AMOUNT = ws_umbrella_rate

def apply_risk_factor_C2(insurance_record: dict[str, Any], ins_claims_count: int) -> None:
    """Apply risk factor."""
    global WS_CALC_AMOUNT
    if ins_claims_count > 2:
        WS_CALC_AMOUNT = WS_CALC_AMOUNT * Decimal("1.25")

def calculate_final_premium_C2(insurance_record: dict[str, Any], ws_total_premiums: Decimal) -> None:
    """Calculate final premium."""
    global WS_CALC_AMOUNT
    insurance_record["premium_amount"]  = None  # TODO: was WS_CALC_AMOUNT
    ws_total_premiums += None  # TODO: was WS_CALC_AMOUNT

def calculate_portfolio_value_C2(investment_master: list[dict[str, Any]], ws_total_investments: Decimal) -> None:
    """Calculate portfolio values."""
    print("CALCULATING PORTFOLIO VALUES...")
    ws_not_eof = True
    ws_eof = False
    while not ws_eof:
        for investment_record in investment_master:
            if not investment_record:
                ws_eof = True
            else:
                calculate_position_value_C2(investment_record)
                calculate_gain_loss_C2(investment_record)
                ws_total_investments = update_totals_C2(investment_record, ws_total_investments)

def calculate_position_value_C2(investment_record: dict[str, Any]) -> None:
    """Calculate position value."""
    investment_record["market_value"] = investment_record["quantity"] * investment_record["current_price"]

def calculate_gain_loss_C2(investment_record: dict[str, Any]) -> None:
    """Calculate gain loss."""
    investment_record["gain_loss"] = investment_record["market_value"] - (investment_record["quantity"] * investment_record["purchase_price"])

def update_totals_C2(investment_record: dict[str, Any], ws_total_investments: Decimal) -> Decimal:
    """Update totals."""
    ws_total_investments += investment_record["market_value"]
    return ws_total_investments

def calculate_dividends_C2(investment_master: list[dict[str, Any]], ws_total_dividends: Decimal) -> None:
    """Calculate dividends."""
    print("CALCULATING DIVIDENDS...")
    ws_not_eof = True
    ws_eof = False
    while not ws_eof:
        for investment_record in investment_master:
            if not investment_record:
                ws_eof = True
            else:
                if investment_record["dividend_rate"] > 0:
                    ws_total_dividends = compute_dividend_C2(investment_record, ws_total_dividends)

def compute_dividend_C2(investment_record: dict[str, Any], ws_total_dividends: Decimal) -> Decimal:
    """COBOL logic"""
    global WS_CALC_AMOUNT
    WS_CALC_AMOUNT = investment_record["market_value"] * investment_record["dividend_rate"] / 4
    ws_total_dividends = post_dividend_C2(ws_total_dividends)
    return ws_total_dividends

def post_dividend_C2(ws_total_dividends: Decimal) -> Decimal:
    """Post dividend."""
    global WS_CALC_AMOUNT
    ws_total_dividends += None  # TODO: was WS_CALC_AMOUNT
    return ws_total_dividends

def write_transaction_C2(ws_current_timestamp: str, ws_calc_amount: Decimal, transaction_record: dict[str, Any]) -> None:
    """Write transaction."""
    transaction_record["timestamp"] = ws_current_timestamp
    transaction_record["type"] = 'DEP'
    transaction_record["amount"] = ws_calc_amount
    transaction_record["status"] = 'C'
    # Assuming a print statement simulates writing to a record
    print(f"Transaction Record: {transaction_record}")

def write_audit_C2(ws_current_timestamp: str, audit_record: dict[str, Any]) -> None:
    """Write audit."""
    audit_record["timestamp"] = ws_current_timestamp
    # Assuming a print statement simulates writing to a record
    print(f"Audit Record: {audit_record}")

def format_date_C2(ws_temp_date: str) -> str:
    """Format date."""
    ws_formatted_date = f"{ws_temp_date[0:4]}-{ws_temp_date[4:6]}-{ws_temp_date[6:8]}"
    return ws_formatted_date

def validate_account_C2(acct_id: str) -> None:
    """Validate account."""
    global WS_VALID, WS_INVALID
    WS_VALID = True
    if acct_id == "":
        WS_INVALID = True

def calculate_tax_C2(ws_calc_amount: Decimal, ws_bracket_1_max: Decimal, ws_bracket_1_rate: Decimal, ws_bracket_2_max: Decimal, ws_bracket_2_rate: Decimal, ws_bracket_3_max: Decimal, ws_bracket_3_rate: Decimal, ws_bracket_5_rate: Decimal) -> None:
    """Calculate tax."""
    global WS_CALC_TAX
    if ws_calc_amount <= ws_bracket_1_max:
        WS_CALC_TAX = ws_calc_amount * ws_bracket_1_rate
    elif ws_calc_amount <= ws_bracket_2_max:
        WS_CALC_TAX = (ws_bracket_1_max * ws_bracket_1_rate) + ((ws_calc_amount - ws_bracket_1_max) * ws_bracket_2_rate)
    elif ws_calc_amount <= ws_bracket_3_max:
        WS_CALC_TAX = (ws_bracket_1_max * ws_bracket_1_rate) + ((ws_bracket_2_max - ws_bracket_1_max) * ws_bracket_2_rate) + ((ws_calc_amount - ws_bracket_2_max) * ws_bracket_3_rate)
    else:
        WS_CALC_TAX = ws_calc_amount * ws_bracket_5_rate

def termination_C2(customer_master: list[dict[str, Any]], account_master: list[dict[str, Any]], loan_master: list[dict[str, Any]], insurance_master: list[dict[str, Any]], investment_master: list[dict[str, Any]], transaction_log: list[dict[str, Any]], audit_trail: list[dict[str, Any]], report_file: list[str], ws_cust_count: int, ws_acct_count: int, ws_tran_count: int, ws_loan_count: int, ws_error_count: int, ws_total_deposits: Decimal, ws_total_withdrawals: Decimal, ws_total_interest: Decimal, ws_total_fees: Decimal, ws_formatted_count: str, ws_formatted_amount: str) -> None:
    """Termination."""
    close_files_C2(customer_master, account_master, loan_master, insurance_master, investment_master, transaction_log, audit_trail, report_file)
    display_statistics_C2(ws_cust_count, ws_acct_count, ws_tran_count, ws_loan_count, ws_error_count, ws_total_deposits, ws_total_withdrawals, ws_total_interest, ws_total_fees, ws_formatted_count, ws_formatted_amount)
    print("mega_enterprise SYSTEM TERMINATED NORMALLY")

def close_files_C2(customer_master: list[dict[str, Any]], account_master: list[dict[str, Any]], loan_master: list[dict[str, Any]], insurance_master: list[dict[str, Any]], investment_master: list[dict[str, Any]], transaction_log: list[dict[str, Any]], audit_trail: list[dict[str, Any]], report_file: list[str]) -> None:
    """Close files."""
    customer_master.clear()
    account_master.clear()
    loan_master.clear()
    insurance_master.clear()
    investment_master.clear()
    transaction_log.clear()
    audit_trail.clear()
    report_file.clear()
    print("Files closed.")

def display_statistics_C2(ws_cust_count: int, ws_acct_count: int, ws_tran_count: int, ws_loan_count: int, ws_error_count: int, ws_total_deposits: Decimal, ws_total_withdrawals: Decimal, ws_total_interest: Decimal, ws_total_fees: Decimal, ws_formatted_count: str, ws_formatted_amount: str) -> None:
    """Display statistics."""

    ws_formatted_count = str(ws_tran_count)
    print("TRANSACTIONS PROCESSED: ", ws_formatted_count)
    ws_formatted_count = str(ws_loan_count)
    print("LOANS PROCESSED:        ", ws_formatted_count)
    ws_formatted_count = str(ws_error_count)
    print("ERRORS ENCOUNTERED:     ", ws_formatted_count)
    print("============================================")
    ws_formatted_amount = str(ws_total_deposits)
    print("TOTAL DEPOSITS:    ", ws_formatted_amount)
    ws_formatted_amount = str(ws_total_withdrawals)
    print("TOTAL WITHDRAWALS: ", ws_formatted_amount)
    ws_formatted_amount = str(ws_total_interest)
    print("TOTAL INTEREST:    ", ws_formatted_amount)
    ws_formatted_amount = str(ws_total_fees)
    print("TOTAL FEES:        ", ws_formatted_amount)
    print("============================================")

def fraud_detection_C2(transaction_log: list[dict[str, Any]], customer_master: list[dict[str, Any]], ws_process_count: int, audit_record: dict[str, Any], ws_current_timestamp: str) -> None:

    geographic_analysis_C2()

def analyze_patterns_C2(transaction_log: list[dict[str, Any]], ws_process_count: int, audit_record: dict[str, Any], ws_current_timestamp: str) -> None:
    """Analyze transaction patterns."""
    print("ANALYZING TRANSACTION PATTERNS...")
    ws_not_eof = True
    ws_eof = False
    while not ws_eof:
        for transaction_record in transaction_log:
            if not transaction_record:
                ws_eof = True
            else:
                check_amount_threshold_C2(transaction_record, ws_process_count, audit_record, ws_current_timestamp)
                check_frequency_C2()
                check_time_pattern_C2()

def check_amount_threshold_C2(transaction_record: dict[str, Any], ws_process_count: int, audit_record: dict[str, Any], ws_current_timestamp: str) -> None:
    """Check amount threshold."""
    if transaction_record["amount"] > 10000:
        flag_large_transaction_C2(ws_process_count, audit_record, ws_current_timestamp)

def flag_large_transaction_C2(ws_process_count: int, audit_record: dict[str, Any], ws_current_timestamp: str) -> None:
    """Flag large transaction."""
    global WS_PROCESS_COUNT
    WS_PROCESS_COUNT += 1
    write_audit_C2(ws_current_timestamp, audit_record)

def behavioral_scoring_C2(customer_master: list[dict[str, Any]]) -> None:
    """Calculating behavioral scores."""
    print("CALCULATING BEHAVIORAL SCORES...")
    ws_not_eof = True
    ws_eof = False
    while not ws_eof:
        for customer_record in customer_master:
            if not customer_record:
                ws_eof = True
            else:
                calculate_risk_score_C2(customer_record)
                update_customer_profile_C2(customer_record)

def calculate_risk_score_C2(customer_record: dict[str, Any]) -> None:
    """Calculate risk score."""
    global WS_CALC_RESULT
    WS_CALC_RESULT = 0
    if customer_record["credit_score"] < 600:
        WS_CALC_RESULT += 30
    if customer_record["total_loans"] > customer_record["total_balance"]:
        WS_CALC_RESULT += 20

def update_customer_profile_C2(customer_record: dict[str, Any]) -> None:
    """Update customer profile."""
    global WS_CALC_RESULT
    if WS_CALC_RESULT > 50:
        customer_record["risk_rating"] = 'H'
    elif WS_CALC_RESULT > 25:
        customer_record["risk_rating"] = 'M'
    else:
        customer_record["risk_rating"] = 'L'

def credit_card_processing_C2(ws_calc_amount: Decimal, acct_overdraft_limit: Decimal, ws_approved: bool, transaction_record: dict[str, Any], acct_balance: Decimal, ws_credit_card_rate: Decimal, ws_total_fees: Decimal) -> None:

    process_settlement_C2()

    generate_statements_C2()

    """Checking credit limit."""
    global WS_NOT_APPROVED, WS_APPROVED
    if ws_calc_amount > acct_overdraft_limit:
        WS_NOT_APPROVED = True
    else:
        WS_APPROVED = True

    """Calculating rewards points."""
    global WS_CALC_RESULT
    WS_CALC_RESULT = tran_amount * Decimal("0.01")
    ws_total_fees += None  # TODO: was WS_CALC_RESULT
    return ws_total_fees

    """Applying credit card interest."""
    global WS_CALC_INTEREST
    WS_CALC_INTEREST = acct_balance * ws_credit_card_rate / 12
    acct_balance += None  # TODO: was WS_CALC_INTEREST
    return acct_balance

def mortgage_processing_C2(loan_payment_amount: Decimal, cust_total_balance: Decimal, loan_current_balance: Decimal, loan_collateral_value: Decimal, cust_credit_score: int, ws_loan_origination_pct: Decimal) -> None:
    """Mortgage processing."""
    process_applications_C2()
    underwriting_C2(loan_payment_amount, cust_total_balance, loan_current_balance, loan_collateral_value, cust_credit_score, ws_loan_origination_pct)

def underwriting_C2(loan_payment_amount: Decimal, cust_total_balance: Decimal, loan_current_balance: Decimal, loan_collateral_value: Decimal, cust_credit_score: int, ws_loan_origination_pct: Decimal) -> None:
    """Performing underwriting."""
    print("PERFORMING UNDERWRITING...")
    dti_calculation_C2(loan_payment_amount, cust_total_balance)
    ltv_calculation_C2(loan_current_balance, loan_collateral_value, ws_loan_origination_pct)
    credit_analysis_C2(cust_credit_score)

def dti_calculation_C2(loan_payment_amount: Decimal, cust_total_balance: Decimal) -> None:
    """DTI calculation."""
    global WS_CALC_RESULT, WS_NOT_APPROVED
    WS_CALC_RESULT = loan_payment_amount / (cust_total_balance / 12)
    if WS_CALC_RESULT > Decimal("0.43"):
        WS_NOT_APPROVED = True

def ltv_calculation_C2(loan_current_balance: Decimal, loan_collateral_value: Decimal, ws_loan_origination_pct: Decimal) -> None:
    """LTV calculation."""
    global LOAN_LTV_RATIO, WS_CALC_FEE
    LOAN_LTV_RATIO = loan_current_balance / loan_collateral_value
    if LOAN_LTV_RATIO > Decimal("0.80"):
        WS_CALC_FEE += ws_loan_origination_pct

def credit_analysis_C2(cust_credit_score: int) -> None:
    """Credit analysis."""
    global WS_NOT_APPROVED
    if cust_credit_score < 620:
        WS_NOT_APPROVED = True

    """Analyzing portfolios."""
    print("ANALYZING PORTFOLIOS...")
    ws_not_eof = True
    ws_eof = False
    while not ws_eof:
        for investment_record in investment_master:

                assess_risk_C2_2(investment_record)
                benchmark_comparison_C2()

def calculate_returns_C2(investment_record: dict[str, Any]) -> None:
    """Calculate returns."""
    global WS_CALC_RESULT
    if investment_record["purchase_price"] > 0:
        WS_CALC_RESULT = (investment_record["current_price"] - investment_record["purchase_price"]) / investment_record["purchase_price"] * 100

def assess_risk_C2_2(investment_record: dict[str, Any]) -> None:
    """Assess risk."""
    global WS_TEMP_FLAG
    if investment_record["type"] == "stocks":
        WS_TEMP_FLAG = 'H'
    elif investment_record["type"] == "bonds":
        WS_TEMP_FLAG = 'L'
    elif investment_record["type"] == "mutual_fund":
        WS_TEMP_FLAG = 'M'
    else:
        WS_TEMP_FLAG = 'M'

def customer_segmentation_C3() -> None:
    """Brief description."""
    print("SEGMENTING CUSTOMERS...")
    global WS_NOT_EOF, WS_EOF
    WS_NOT_EOF = True
    while not WS_EOF:
        try:
            customer_master_next_C3()
        except StopIteration:
            WS_EOF = True
        else:
            calculate_clv_C3()
            assign_segment_C3()

def customer_master_next_C3() -> CustomerMasterC3:
  """Simulates reading next record, raises StopIteration at end."""
  global CUSTOMER_MASTER
  if not hasattr(customer_master_next_C3, "index"):
      customer_master_next_C3.index = 0
  
  # Assuming a list of customer records
  customer_records = [
      CustomerMasterC3(Decimal("1000"), Decimal("200"), Decimal("5000")),
      CustomerMasterC3(Decimal("6000"), Decimal("1000"), Decimal("10000")),
      CustomerMasterC3(Decimal("100"), Decimal("500"), Decimal("100")),
      CustomerMasterC3(Decimal("20000"), Decimal("500"), Decimal("10000"))
  ]
  
  if customer_master_next_C3.index < len(customer_records):
      record = customer_records[customer_master_next_C3.index]
      CUSTOMER_MASTER = record
      customer_master_next_C3.index += 1
      return record
  else:
      raise StopIteration

def calculate_clv_C3() -> None:
    """Brief description."""
    global WS_CALC_RESULT
    WS_CALC_RESULT = (CUSTOMER_MASTER.cust_total_balance * WS_SAVINGS_RATE) + (CUSTOMER_MASTER.cust_total_loans * WS_PERSONAL_RATE) + (CUSTOMER_MASTER.cust_total_investments * Decimal("0.01"))

def assign_segment_C3() -> None:
    """Brief description."""
    global WS_TEMP_CODE
    if WS_CALC_RESULT > 10000:
        WS_TEMP_CODE = 'PLATINUM'
    elif WS_CALC_RESULT > 5000:
        WS_TEMP_CODE = 'GOLD'
    elif WS_CALC_RESULT > 1000:
        WS_TEMP_CODE = 'SILVER'
    else:
        WS_TEMP_CODE = 'BRONZE'

            global WS_PROCESS_COUNT
            WS_PROCESS_COUNT += 1

@dataclass
class Customer:
    cust_status: Optional[str] = None
    cust_ssn: Optional[str] = None
    cust_credit_score: Optional[int] = None
    cust_risk_rating: Optional[str] = None
    cust_total_balance: Optional[Decimal] = None
    cust_total_loans: Optional[Decimal] = None

@dataclass
class TransactionLog:
    tran_amount: Optional[Decimal] = None

ws_temp_code: Optional[str] = None
ws_calc_result: Optional[Decimal] = None
ws_total_deposits: Optional[Decimal] = None
ws_total_loans: Optional[Decimal] = None
ws_calc_amount: Optional[Decimal] = None
ws_total_fees: Optional[Decimal] = None
ws_not_eof: bool = False
ws_eof: bool = False
ws_process_count: int = 0
ws_error_count: int = 0
ws_current_timestamp: Optional[str] = None
ws_temp_string: Optional[str] = None
ws_valid: bool = False
ws_atm_fee_foreign: Optional[Decimal] = None
ws_formatted_count: Optional[str] = None
ws_cust_count: int = 0

loan_current_balance: Optional[Decimal] = None
loan_paid_off: bool = False

customer_C4 = Customer()
transaction_log_C4 = TransactionLog()

        ws_temp_code = 'CONFIDENTIAL'

def c100_transaction_monitoring_C4() -> None:
    """Monitoring transactions."""
    global ws_not_eof, ws_eof
    print("MONITORING TRANSACTIONS...")
    ws_not_eof = True
    while not ws_eof:
        # Simulate reading transaction log
        # For demonstration, let's just break the loop after one iteration'
        ws_eof = True  # In real code, this would depend on the read result
        c110_rule_based_detection_C4()
        c120_behavior_analysis_C4()
        c130_network_analysis_C4()

def c110_rule_based_detection_C4() -> None:
    """Rule-based detection."""
    if transaction_log_C4.tran_amount is not None and transaction_log_C4.tran_amount >= 10000:
        c111_flag_ctr_C4()
    if transaction_log_C4.tran_amount is not None and 5000 <= transaction_log_C4.tran_amount < 10000:
        c112_check_structuring_C4()

def c111_flag_ctr_C4() -> None:
    """Flag CTR."""
    global ws_process_count
    ws_process_count += 1

def c112_check_structuring_C4() -> None:
    """Check structuring."""
    global ws_error_count
    ws_error_count += 1

def d110_classification_C4() -> None:
    """Classification."""
    if customer_C4.cust_credit_score is not None:
        if customer_C4.cust_credit_score > 750:
            customer_C4.cust_risk_rating = 'A'
        elif customer_C4.cust_credit_score > 650:
            customer_C4.cust_risk_rating = 'B'
        elif customer_C4.cust_credit_score > 550:
            customer_C4.cust_risk_rating = 'C'
        else:
            customer_C4.cust_risk_rating = 'D'

def d120_regression_C4() -> None:
    """Regression."""
    global ws_calc_result
    if customer_C4.cust_credit_score is not None and customer_C4.cust_total_balance is not None and customer_C4.cust_total_loans is not None:
        ws_calc_result = (customer_C4.cust_credit_score * 10) + (customer_C4.cust_total_balance / 1000) - (customer_C4.cust_total_loans / 2000)

def main_loop_C5() -> None:
    """Main processing loop."""
    global WS_NOT_EOF_C5, WS_EOF_C5, WS_CUST_COUNT_C5
    WS_NOT_EOF_C5 = True
    while not WS_EOF_C5:
        try:
            customer = CUSTOMER_MASTER_C5.pop(0)
            update_profile_C5()
            enrich_profile_C5()
            WS_CUST_COUNT_C5 += 1
        except IndexError:
            WS_EOF_C5 = True

def process_transactions_2000_C5() -> None:
    """Process transactions from the input file."""
    global WS_EOF_FLAG_C5, WS_TRANS_COUNT_C5
    try:
        WS_TRANSACTION_REC_C5 = "DUMMY"  # Simulate reading transaction data
        WS_TRANS_COUNT_C5 += 1
        validate_transaction_2100_C5()
        if WS_VALID_FLAG_C5 == 'Y':
            process_by_type_2200_C5()
        else:
            handle_error_2900_C5()
    except Exception: # simulate EOF
        WS_EOF_FLAG_C5 = 'Y'

def validate_transaction_2100_C5() -> None:
    """Validate transaction record."""
    global WS_VALID_FLAG_C5, WS_ERROR_MSG_C5, TXN_ACCOUNT_ID_C5, TXN_AMOUNT_C5, TXN_TYPE_C5
    WS_VALID_FLAG_C5 = 'Y'
    if TXN_ACCOUNT_ID_C5 == '' or TXN_ACCOUNT_ID_C5 is None:
        WS_VALID_FLAG_C5 = 'N'
        WS_ERROR_MSG_C5 = 'INVALID ACCOUNT ID'
        return  # EXIT PARAGRAPH
    try:
        float(TXN_AMOUNT_C5)  # check if numeric
    except ValueError:
        WS_VALID_FLAG_C5 = 'N'
        WS_ERROR_MSG_C5 = 'INVALID AMOUNT'
        return # EXIT PARAGRAPH

    valid_types = ['D', 'W', 'T', 'I']
    if TXN_TYPE_C5 not in valid_types:
        WS_VALID_FLAG_C5 = 'N'
        WS_ERROR_MSG_C5 = 'INVALID TRANSACTION TYPE'

    validate_account_exists_2150_C5()
    validate_business_rules_2160_C5()

def validate_account_exists_2150_C5() -> None:
    """Validate that the account exists."""
    global WS_SEARCH_KEY_C5, TXN_ACCOUNT_ID_C5
    WS_SEARCH_KEY_C5  = None  # TODO: was TXN_ACCOUNT_ID_C5
    search_account_5000_C5()
    if WS_FOUND_FLAG_C5 == 'N':
        WS_VALID_FLAG_C5 = 'N'
        WS_ERROR_MSG_C5 = 'ACCOUNT NOT FOUND'

def validate_business_rules_2160_C5() -> None:
    """Validate business rules."""
    global WS_VALID_FLAG_C5, WS_ERROR_MSG_C5, TXN_TYPE_C5, TXN_AMOUNT_C5, WS_ACCOUNT_BALANCE_C5
    if TXN_TYPE_C5 == 'W':
        try:
            amount = Decimal(TXN_AMOUNT_C5)
            balance = Decimal(WS_ACCOUNT_BALANCE_C5)
            if amount > balance:
                WS_VALID_FLAG_C5 = 'N'
                WS_ERROR_MSG_C5 = 'INSUFFICIENT FUNDS'
        except:
            pass #Handle error, though already validated in 2100

    try:
        if Decimal(TXN_AMOUNT_C5) > 1000000:
            WS_VALID_FLAG_C5 = 'N'
            WS_ERROR_MSG_C5 = 'AMOUNT EXCEEDS LIMIT'
    except:
        pass #Handle error, though already validated in 2100

def process_by_type_2200_C5() -> None:
    """Process transaction based on type."""
    global TXN_TYPE_C5
    if TXN_TYPE_C5 == 'D':
        process_deposit_2300_C5()
    elif TXN_TYPE_C5 == 'W':
        process_withdrawal_2400_C5()
    elif TXN_TYPE_C5 == 'T':
        process_transfer_2500_C5()
    elif TXN_TYPE_C5 == 'I':
        process_interest_2600_C5()
    else:
        handle_error_2900_C5()

def process_deposit_2300_C5() -> None:

        pass #Handle error - already checked if number

def process_withdrawal_2400_C5() -> None:
    """Process a withdrawal transaction."""
    global WS_ACCOUNT_BALANCE_C5, TXN_AMOUNT_C5, WS_TXN_DESC_C5, WS_TOTAL_WITHDRAWALS_C5, WS_WITHDRAWAL_COUNT_C5, WS_MIN_BALANCE_LIMIT_C5
    try:
        WS_ACCOUNT_BALANCE_C5 = str(Decimal(WS_ACCOUNT_BALANCE_C5) - Decimal(TXN_AMOUNT_C5)) #SUBTRACT txn_amount FROM ws_account_balance
        WS_TXN_DESC_C5 = 'WITHDRAWAL'
        WS_TOTAL_WITHDRAWALS_C5 = str(Decimal(WS_TOTAL_WITHDRAWALS_C5) + Decimal(TXN_AMOUNT_C5))#ADD txn_amount TO ws_total_withdrawals
        WS_WITHDRAWAL_COUNT_C5 += 1
        update_account_2350_C5()
        write_audit_trail_2380_C5()
        if Decimal(WS_ACCOUNT_BALANCE_C5) < Decimal(WS_MIN_BALANCE_LIMIT_C5):
            generate_low_balance_alert_2450_C5()
    except:
        pass #Handle error - already checked if number

    ALERT_ACCOUNT_C5  = None  # TODO: was TXN_ACCOUNT_ID_C5
    ALERT_BALANCE_C5 = WS_ACCOUNT_BALANCE_C5
    ALERT_DATE_C5 = "CURRENTDATE"  # MOVE FUNCTION current_date TO alert_date
    ALERT_RECORD_C5  = None  # TODO: was WS_ALERT_RECORD_C5
    WS_ALERT_COUNT_C5 += 1
    #WRITE alert_record FROM ws_alert_record

def process_transfer_2500_C5() -> None:
    """Process a transfer transaction."""
    validate_target_account_2510_C5()
    if WS_VALID_FLAG_C5 == 'Y':
        debit_source_2520_C5()
        credit_target_2530_C5()
        record_transfer_2540_C5()
    else:
        handle_error_2900_C5()

def validate_target_account_2510_C5() -> None:
    """Validate that the target account exists."""
    global WS_SEARCH_KEY_C5, TXN_TARGET_ACCOUNT_C5
    WS_SEARCH_KEY_C5 = TXN_TARGET_ACCOUNT_C5
    search_account_5000_C5()
    if WS_FOUND_FLAG_C5 == 'N':
        WS_VALID_FLAG_C5 = 'N'
        WS_ERROR_MSG_C5 = 'TARGET ACCOUNT NOT FOUND'

def debit_source_2520_C5() -> None:
    """Debit the source account."""
    global WS_SOURCE_BALANCE_C5, TXN_AMOUNT_C5, ACCT_BALANCE_C5, ACCOUNT_RECORD_C5
    #Source account is WS_ACCOUNT_BALANCE_C5?  Must store and set it from the search function in 2150
    WS_SOURCE_BALANCE_C5 = str(Decimal(WS_SOURCE_BALANCE_C5) - Decimal(TXN_AMOUNT_C5))
    ACCT_BALANCE_C5 = WS_SOURCE_BALANCE_C5
    #REWRITE account_record

    global WS_TARGET_BALANCE_C5

def calculate_simple_interest_C6() -> None:
    """Calculate simple interest."""
    global WS_SIMPLE_INTEREST_C6, WS_ACCOUNT_BALANCE_C6, WS_INTEREST_RATE_C6, WS_DAYS_IN_PERIOD_C6
    WS_SIMPLE_INTEREST_C6 = (WS_ACCOUNT_BALANCE_C6 * WS_INTEREST_RATE_C6 * WS_DAYS_IN_PERIOD_C6) / 36500

def calculate_compound_interest_C6() -> None:
    """Calculate compound interest."""
    global WS_COMPOUND_FACTOR_C6, WS_INTEREST_RATE_C6, WS_DAYS_IN_PERIOD_C6, WS_COMPOUND_INTEREST_C6, WS_ACCOUNT_BALANCE_C6
    WS_COMPOUND_FACTOR_C6 = (1 + WS_INTEREST_RATE_C6 / 36500) ** WS_DAYS_IN_PERIOD_C6
    WS_COMPOUND_INTEREST_C6 = WS_ACCOUNT_BALANCE_C6 * (WS_COMPOUND_FACTOR_C6 - 1)

def apply_interest_C6() -> None:
    """Apply interest."""
    global WS_INTEREST_METHOD_C6, WS_SIMPLE_INTEREST_C6, WS_ACCOUNT_BALANCE_C6, WS_COMPOUND_INTEREST_C6
    if WS_INTEREST_METHOD_C6 == 'S':
        WS_ACCOUNT_BALANCE_C6 += WS_SIMPLE_INTEREST_C6
    else:
        WS_ACCOUNT_BALANCE_C6 += WS_COMPOUND_INTEREST_C6
    update_account_C6()

def fee_processing_C6() -> None:
    """Fee processing."""
    calculate_monthly_fee_C6()
    calculate_transaction_fees_C6()
    apply_fee_waivers_C6()
    deduct_fees_C6()

def calculate_monthly_fee_C6() -> None:
    """Calculate monthly fee."""
    global WS_ACCOUNT_TYPE_C6, WS_MONTHLY_FEE_C6
    if WS_ACCOUNT_TYPE_C6 == 'CHK':
        WS_MONTHLY_FEE_C6 = 12.00
    elif WS_ACCOUNT_TYPE_C6 == 'SAV':
        WS_MONTHLY_FEE_C6 = 5.00
    elif WS_ACCOUNT_TYPE_C6 == 'PRM':
        WS_MONTHLY_FEE_C6 = 25.00
    else:
        WS_MONTHLY_FEE_C6 = 0.00

def calculate_transaction_fees_C6() -> None:
    """Calculate transaction fees."""
    global WS_TRANS_COUNT_C6, WS_FREE_TRANS_LIMIT_C6, WS_EXCESS_TRANS_C6, WS_TRANS_FEE_C6, WS_PER_TRANS_FEE_C6
    if WS_TRANS_COUNT_C6 > WS_FREE_TRANS_LIMIT_C6:
        WS_EXCESS_TRANS_C6 = WS_TRANS_COUNT_C6 - WS_FREE_TRANS_LIMIT_C6
        WS_TRANS_FEE_C6 = WS_EXCESS_TRANS_C6 * WS_PER_TRANS_FEE_C6
    else:
        WS_TRANS_FEE_C6 = 0

def apply_fee_waivers_C6() -> None:
    """Apply fee waivers."""
    global WS_ACCOUNT_BALANCE_C6, WS_MIN_BALANCE_WAIVER_C6, WS_MONTHLY_FEE_C6, WS_CUSTOMER_TIER_C6, WS_TRANS_FEE_C6
    if WS_ACCOUNT_BALANCE_C6 >= WS_MIN_BALANCE_WAIVER_C6:
        WS_MONTHLY_FEE_C6 = 0
    if WS_CUSTOMER_TIER_C6 == 'GOLD' or WS_CUSTOMER_TIER_C6 == 'PLATINUM':
        WS_TRANS_FEE_C6 = WS_TRANS_FEE_C6 * 0.5

def deduct_fees_C6() -> None:
    """Deduct fees."""
    global WS_MONTHLY_FEE_C6, WS_TRANS_FEE_C6, WS_TOTAL_FEES_C6, WS_ACCOUNT_BALANCE_C6
    WS_TOTAL_FEES_C6 = WS_MONTHLY_FEE_C6 + WS_TRANS_FEE_C6
    WS_ACCOUNT_BALANCE_C6 -= None  # TODO: was WS_TOTAL_FEES_C6
    update_account_C6()
    record_fee_transaction_C6()

def record_fee_transaction_C6() -> None:
    """Record fee transaction."""
    global WS_FEE_RECORD_C6, TXN_ACCOUNT_ID_C6, FEE_ACCOUNT_C6, WS_TOTAL_FEES_C6, FEE_AMOUNT_C6, FEE_DESCRIPTION_C6, FEE_DATE_C6
    WS_FEE_RECORD_C6 = {}
    FEE_ACCOUNT_C6  = None  # TODO: was TXN_ACCOUNT_ID_C6
    FEE_AMOUNT_C6  = None  # TODO: was WS_TOTAL_FEES_C6
    FEE_DESCRIPTION_C6 = 'MONTHLY FEE'
    # Replace with Python equivalent of current_date
    FEE_DATE_C6 = "CURRENT_DATE_PLACEHOLDER"
    write_fee_record_C6()

def write_fee_record_C6() -> None:

            create_amortization_C6()
            finalize_loan_C6()
        else:
            process_decline_C6()

def validate_loan_application_C6() -> None:
    """Validate loan application."""
    global WS_VALID_FLAG_C6, WS_LOAN_AMOUNT_C6, WS_ERROR_MSG_C6, WS_LOAN_TERM_MONTHS_C6
    WS_VALID_FLAG_C6 = 'Y'
    if WS_LOAN_AMOUNT_C6 < 1000:
        WS_VALID_FLAG_C6 = 'N'
        WS_ERROR_MSG_C6 = 'MINIMUM LOAN AMOUNT IS $1000'
        return
    if WS_LOAN_AMOUNT_C6 > 10000000:
        WS_VALID_FLAG_C6 = 'N'
        WS_ERROR_MSG_C6 = 'MAXIMUM LOAN AMOUNT EXCEEDED'
        return
    if WS_LOAN_TERM_MONTHS_C6 < 6 or WS_LOAN_TERM_MONTHS_C6 > 360:
        WS_VALID_FLAG_C6 = 'N'
        WS_ERROR_MSG_C6 = 'INVALID LOAN TERM'

    """Score payment history

def calculate_pmi_C7(ws_ltv_ratio: Decimal, ws_loan_amount: Decimal) -> Decimal:
    """Calculates PMI amount."""
    if ws_ltv_ratio > 95:
        ws_pmi_amount = ws_loan_amount * Decimal('0.0125') / 12
    elif ws_ltv_ratio > 90:
        ws_pmi_amount = ws_loan_amount * Decimal('0.0100') / 12
    elif ws_ltv_ratio > 85:
        ws_pmi_amount = ws_loan_amount * Decimal('0.0075') / 12
    else:
        ws_pmi_amount = ws_loan_amount * Decimal('0.0050') / 12
    return ws_pmi_amount

def evaluate_history_C7(ws_late_90_days: int, ws_late_60_days: int, ws_late_30_days: int, ws_risk_score: int) -> tuple[int, str, str, str]:
    """Evaluates credit history and adjusts risk score."""
    ws_factor_1 = ""
    ws_factor_2 = ""
    ws_factor_3 = ""
    if ws_late_90_days > 0:
        ws_risk_score -= 50
        ws_factor_1 = 'SEVERE DELINQUENCY HISTORY'
    if ws_late_60_days > 2:
        ws_risk_score -= 30
        ws_factor_2 = '60+ DAY DELINQUENCIES'
    if ws_late_30_days > 5:
        ws_risk_score -= 20
        ws_factor_3 = 'MULTIPLE 30-DAY LATES'
    return ws_risk_score, ws_factor_1, ws_factor_2, ws_factor_3

def calculate_final_risk_C7(ws_risk_score: Decimal) -> tuple[Decimal, str]:
    """Calculates final risk and assigns a risk category."""
    ws_risk_score = ws_risk_score / 4
    ws_risk_category = ""
    if ws_risk_score >= 80:
        ws_risk_category = 'LOW RISK'
    elif ws_risk_score >= 60:
        ws_risk_category = 'MODERATE'
    elif ws_risk_score >= 40:
        ws_risk_category = 'ELEVATED'
    else:
        ws_risk_category = 'HIGH RISK'
    return ws_risk_score, ws_risk_category

def determine_approval_C7(ws_credit_tier: str, ws_risk_category: str, ws_dti_ratio: Decimal, ws_loan_amount: Decimal, ws_base_rate: Decimal) -> tuple[str, str, Decimal, Decimal]:
    """Determines loan approval status."""
    ws_approval_status = ""
    ws_conditions = ""
    ws_approved_amount = Decimal("0.00")
    ws_approved_rate = Decimal("0.00")
    if ws_credit_tier == 'F':
        ws_approval_status = 'D'
        ws_conditions = 'CREDIT SCORE TOO LOW'
        return ws_approval_status, ws_conditions, ws_approved_amount, ws_approved_rate
    if ws_risk_category == 'HIGH RISK':
        ws_approval_status = 'D'
        ws_conditions = 'RISK ASSESSMENT FAILED'
        return ws_approval_status, ws_conditions, ws_approved_amount, ws_approved_rate
    if ws_dti_ratio > 50:
        ws_approval_status = 'D'
        ws_conditions = 'DTI RATIO TOO HIGH'
        return ws_approval_status, ws_conditions, ws_approved_amount, ws_approved_rate
    ws_approval_status = 'A'
    ws_approved_amount, ws_approved_rate = calculate_approved_terms_C7(ws_loan_amount, ws_base_rate, ws_credit_tier, ws_risk_category)
    return ws_approval_status, ws_conditions, ws_approved_amount, ws_approved_rate

def calculate_approved_terms_C7(ws_loan_amount: Decimal, ws_base_rate: Decimal, ws_credit_tier: str, ws_risk_category: str) -> tuple[Decimal, Decimal]:
    """Calculates approved loan terms."""
    ws_approved_amount = ws_loan_amount
    ws_approved_rate = Decimal("0.00")
    if ws_credit_tier == 'A':
        ws_approved_rate = ws_base_rate + Decimal("0.00")
    elif ws_credit_tier == 'B':
        ws_approved_rate = ws_base_rate + Decimal("0.50")
    elif ws_credit_tier == 'C':
        ws_approved_rate = ws_base_rate + Decimal("1.50")
    elif ws_credit_tier == 'D':
        ws_approved_rate = ws_base_rate + Decimal("3.00")
    if ws_risk_category == 'ELEVATED':
        ws_approved_rate += Decimal("0.50")
    return ws_approved_amount, ws_approved_rate

def generate_loan_terms_C7(ws_approved_rate: Decimal, ws_loan_term_months: int, ws_loan_amount: Decimal) -> tuple[Decimal, Decimal, Decimal, Decimal]:
    """Generates loan terms."""
    ws_loan_interest_rate = ws_approved_rate
    ws_monthly_rate = ws_loan_interest_rate / 1200
    ws_compound_factor = (1 + ws_monthly_rate) ** ws_loan_term_months
    ws_loan_monthly_pmt = ws_loan_amount * ws_monthly_rate * ws_compound_factor / (ws_compound_factor - 1)
    ws_loan_principal_bal = ws_loan_amount
    return ws_loan_interest_rate, ws_monthly_rate, ws_compound_factor, ws_loan_monthly_pmt

def create_amortization_C7(ws_loan_amount: Decimal, ws_loan_term_months: int, ws_monthly_rate: Decimal, ws_loan_monthly_pmt: Decimal, ws_property_tax: Decimal, ws_insurance_premium: Decimal, ws_pmi_amount: Decimal, loan_mortgage: bool) -> tuple[List[Decimal], List[Decimal], List[Decimal], List[int], List[Decimal], List[Decimal]]:
    """Creates amortization schedule."""
    ws_running_balance = ws_loan_amount
    ws_payment_date = "current_date"  # Assuming current date is needed
    amort_interest: List[Decimal] = []
    amort_principal: List[Decimal] = []
    amort_balance: List[Decimal] = []
    amort_payment_num: List[int] = []
    amort_payment_amt: List[Decimal] = []
    amort_escrow: List[Decimal] = []
    amort_total_pmt: List[Decimal] = []

    ws_payment_month: int = 1
    ws_payment_year: int = 2024 # TODO: Replace with current year

    for ws_amort_idx in range(1, ws_loan_term_months + 1):
        interest, principal, balance, payment_num, payment_amt, escrow, total_pmt, ws_payment_month, ws_payment_year = calculate_payment_split_C7(ws_running_balance, ws_monthly_rate, ws_loan_monthly_pmt, ws_amort_idx, ws_property_tax, ws_insurance_premium, ws_pmi_amount, loan_mortgage, ws_payment_month, ws_payment_year)
        amort_interest.append(interest)
        amort_principal.append(principal)
        amort_balance.append(balance)
        amort_payment_num.append(payment_num)
        amort_payment_amt.append(payment_amt)
        amort_escrow.append(escrow)
        amort_total_pmt.append(total_pmt)
        ws_running_balance = balance
        
    return amort_interest, amort_principal, amort_balance, amort_payment_num, amort_payment_amt, amort_total_pmt

def calculate_payment_split_C7(ws_running_balance: Decimal, ws_monthly_rate: Decimal, ws_loan_monthly_pmt: Decimal, ws_amort_idx: int, ws_property_tax: Decimal, ws_insurance_premium: Decimal, ws_pmi_amount: Decimal, loan_mortgage: bool, ws_payment_month: int, ws_payment_year: int) -> tuple[Decimal, Decimal, Decimal, int, Decimal, Decimal, Decimal, int, int]:
    """Calculates payment split for amortization."""
    amort_interest = ws_running_balance * ws_monthly_rate
    amort_principal = ws_loan_monthly_pmt - amort_interest
    ws_running_balance -= amort_principal
    amort_balance = ws_running_balance
    amort_payment_num = ws_amort_idx
    amort_payment_amt = ws_loan_monthly_pmt
    
    if loan_mortgage:
        amort_escrow = (ws_property_tax + ws_insurance_premium) / 12
        amort_total_pmt = ws_loan_monthly_pmt + amort_escrow + ws_pmi_amount
    else:
        amort_escrow = Decimal("0.00")
        amort_total_pmt = ws_loan_monthly_pmt
        
    ws_payment_month, ws_payment_year = advance_payment_date_C7(ws_payment_month, ws_payment_year)
    
    return amort_interest, amort_principal, amort_balance, amort_payment_num, amort_payment_amt, amort_escrow, amort_total_pmt, ws_payment_month, ws_payment_year

def advance_payment_date_C7(ws_payment_month: int, ws_payment_year: int) -> tuple[int, int]:
    """Advances payment date."""
    ws_payment_month += 1
    if ws_payment_month > 12:
        ws_payment_month = 1
        ws_payment_year += 1
    return ws_payment_month, ws_payment_year

def finalize_loan_C7(ws_loan_term_months: int, ws_loan_id: str, ws_loan_type: str, ws_loan_amount: Decimal, ws_loan_interest_rate: Decimal, ws_loan_monthly_pmt: Decimal, ws_loan_record: Any) -> None:
    """Finalizes loan processing."""
    ws_loan_start_date = "current_date"  # Assuming current date is needed
    ws_loan_end_date = ws_loan_start_date + (ws_loan_term_months * 30)
    ws_loan_status = 'A'
    create_loan_record_C7(ws_loan_id, ws_loan_type, ws_loan_amount, ws_loan_interest_rate, ws_loan_monthly_pmt, ws_loan_start_date, ws_loan_status, ws_loan_record)
    disburse_funds_C7(ws_loan_amount)
    send_confirmation_C7()

def load_portfolio_C7() -> tuple[List[Any], int]:
    """Loads portfolio holdings from file."""
    ws_hold_idx = 1
    ws_eof_flag = 'N'
    ws_holding: List[Any] = []
    ws_holding_rec: Any = None # TODO: Figure out holding record
    while ws_hold_idx <= 100 and ws_eof_flag == 'N':
        # READ holdings_file INTO ws_holding_rec
        try:
            # ws_holding_rec = read_holdings_file() # Placeholder for reading file
            ws_holding.append(ws_holding_rec)
            ws_hold_idx += 1
        except: # EOFError: # Assuming EOFError is raised at end of file
            ws_eof_flag = 'Y'
    ws_holdings_count = ws_hold_idx - 1
    return ws_holding, ws_holdings_count

def update_market_prices_C7(ws_holdings_count: int, ws_holding: List[Any]) -> None:
    """Updates market prices for holdings."""
    for ws_hold_idx in range(0, ws_holdings_count):
        ws_quote_symbol = ws_holding[ws_hold_idx].hold_symbol
        ws_quote_price = get_quote_C7(ws_quote_symbol)
        ws_holding[ws_hold_idx].hold_current_price = ws_quote_price

def get_quote_C7(ws_quote_symbol: str) -> Decimal:
    """Gets market quote for a symbol."""
    quote_request_symbol = ws_quote_symbol
    # CALL 'GETQUOTE' USING quote_request quote_response
    quote_response_status = 'OK' # TODO: Replace with actual call

    if quote_response_status == 'OK':
        quote_last_price = Decimal("100.00")
        ws_quote_price = quote_last_price
    else:
        ws_quote_price = Decimal("0.00")
    return ws_quote_price

def calculate_values_C7(ws_holdings_count: int, ws_holding: List[Any]) -> tuple[Decimal, Decimal, Decimal]:
    """Calculates portfolio values."""
    ws_total_value = Decimal("0.00")
    ws_cost_basis = Decimal("0.00")
    ws_unrealized_gain = Decimal("0.00")
    for ws_hold_idx in range(0, ws_holdings_count):
        ws_total_value, ws_cost_basis, ws_unrealized_gain = calculate_holding_value_C7(ws_total_value, ws_cost_basis, ws_unrealized_gain, ws_holding[ws_hold_idx].hold_shares, ws_holding[ws_hold_idx].hold_current_price, ws_holding[ws_hold_idx].hold_cost_per_share)
    return ws_total_value, ws_cost_basis, ws_unrealized_gain

def calculate_holding_value_C7(ws_total_value: Decimal, ws_cost_basis: Decimal, ws_unrealized_gain: Decimal, hold_shares: Decimal, hold_current_price: Decimal, hold_cost_per_share: Decimal) -> tuple[Decimal, Decimal, Decimal]:
    """Calculates value for a single holding."""
    hold_market_value = hold_shares * hold_current_price
    ws_hold_cost = hold_shares * hold_cost_per_share
    hold_gain_loss = hold_market_value - ws_hold_cost
    if ws_hold_cost > 0:
        hold_pct_change = (hold_gain_loss / ws_hold_cost) * 100
    else:
        hold_pct_change = Decimal("0.00")
    ws_total_value += hold_market_value
    ws_cost_basis += ws_hold_cost
    ws_unrealized_gain += hold_gain_loss
    return ws_total_value, ws_cost_basis, ws_unrealized_gain

def rebalance_check_C7(ws_total_value: Decimal, ws_holding: List[Any], ws_holdings_count: int) -> None:
    """Checks if rebalancing is needed."""
    ws_stocks_pct, ws_bonds_pct, ws_cash_pct = calculate_current_allocation_C7(ws_total_value, ws_holding, ws_holdings_count)
    ws_rebalance_needed = compare_to_target_C7(ws_stocks_pct, ws_bonds_pct)
    if ws_rebalance_needed == 'Y':
        generate_rebalance_trades_C7(ws_total_value, ws_stocks_pct)

def calculate_current_allocation_C7(ws_total_value: Decimal, ws_holding: List[Any], ws_holdings_count: int) -> tuple[Decimal, Decimal, Decimal]:
    """Calculates current asset allocation."""
    ws_stocks_value = Decimal("0.00")
    ws_bonds_value = Decimal("0.00")
    ws_cash_value = Decimal("0.00")
    for ws_hold_idx in range(0, ws_holdings_count):
        hold_type = ws_holding[ws_hold_idx].hold_type
        hold_market_value = ws_holding[ws_hold_idx].hold_market_value
        if hold_type == 'STK':
            ws_stocks_value += hold_market_value
        elif hold_type == 'BND':
            ws_bonds_value += hold_market_value
        elif hold_type == 'CSH':
            ws_cash_value += hold_market_value
    ws_stocks_pct = (ws_stocks_value / ws_total_value) * 100
    ws_bonds_pct = (ws_bonds_value / ws_total_value) * 100
    ws_cash_pct = (ws_cash_value / ws_total_value) * 100
    return ws_stocks_pct, ws_bonds_pct, ws_cash_pct

def compare_to_target_C7(ws_stocks_pct: Decimal, ws_bonds_pct: Decimal) -> str:
    """Compares current allocation to target."""
    ws_target_stocks_pct = Decimal("50.00") # TODO: Replace with appropriate value
    ws_target_bonds_pct = Decimal("30.00") # TODO: Replace with appropriate value
    ws_rebalance_needed = 'N'
    ws_stocks_diff = ws_stocks_pct - ws_target_stocks_pct
    ws_bonds_diff = ws_bonds_pct - ws_target_bonds_pct
    if abs(ws_stocks_diff) > 5:
        ws_rebalance_needed = 'Y'
    if abs(ws_bonds_diff) > 5:
        ws_rebalance_needed = 'Y'
    return ws_rebalance_needed

def generate_rebalance_trades_C7(ws_total_value: Decimal, ws_stocks_pct: Decimal) -> None:
    """Generates rebalancing trades."""
    ws_target_stocks_pct = Decimal("50.00") # TODO: Replace with appropriate value
    ws_stocks_diff = ws_stocks_pct - ws_target_stocks_pct
    if ws_stocks_diff > 0:
        ws_sell_amount = ws_total_value * ws_stocks_diff / 100
        create_sell_order_C7(ws_sell_amount)
    else:
        ws_buy_amount = ws_total_value * (0 - ws_stocks_diff) / 100
        create_buy_order_C7(ws_buy_amount)

def create_sell_order_C7(ws_sell_amount: Decimal) -> None:
    """Creates a sell order."""
    ws_trade_type = 'SELL'
    ws_order_type = 'MARKET'
    ws_trade_amount = ws_sell_amount
    trade_execution_C7()

    """Creates a buy order."""
    ws_trade_type = 'BUY '
    ws_order_type = 'MARKET'
    ws_trade_amount = ws_buy_amount
    trade_execution_C7()

    ws_total_value = Decimal("100000.00")
    ws_quarter_start_value = Decimal("90000.00")
    rpt_quarter_return = (ws_total_value - ws_quarter_start_value) / ws_quarter_start_value * 100
    # WRITE report_record FROM ws_performance_line
    pass

    rpt_title = 'ANNUAL TAX REPORT - 1099'
    ws_dividend_income = Decimal("1000.00")
    ws_realized_gain_ytd = Decimal("5000.00")
    # WRITE report_record FROM ws_tax_line

def trade_execution_C7() -> None:
    """Executes a trade."""
    validate_order_C7()
    ws_order_valid = 'Y' # TODO: Remove default and get the result from validate_order_C7
    if ws_order_valid == 'Y':
        check_funds_shares_C7()
        ws_sufficient_flag = 'Y' # TODO: Remove default and get result from check_funds_shares_C7
        if ws_sufficient_flag == 'Y':
            route_order_C7()
            execute_order_C7()
            settle_trade_C7()
        else:
            reject_order_C7()

def validate_order_C7() -> None:
    """Validates a trade order."""
    ws_order_valid = 'Y'
    ws_trade_symbol = "AAPL" # TODO: Real value
    ws_trade_shares = 10 # TODO: Real value
    order_limit = False # TODO: Real Value
    order_stop_limit = False # TODO: Real value
    ws_limit_price = Decimal("150.00") # TODO: Real value
    if not ws_trade_symbol:
        ws_order_valid = 'N'
        ws_reject_reason = 'SYMBOL REQUIRED'
        return
    if ws_trade_shares <= 0:
        ws_order_valid = 'N'
        ws_reject_reason = 'INVALID QUANTITY'
        return
    if order_limit or order_stop_limit:
        if ws_limit_price <= 0:
            ws_order_valid = 'N'
            ws_reject_reason = 'LIMIT PRICE REQUIRED'

def check_funds_shares_C7() -> None:
    """Checks if sufficient funds or shares are available."""
    ws_sufficient_flag = 'Y'
    trade_buy = True # TODO: Real value
    trade_sell = False # TODO: Real value
    ws_available_cash = Decimal("10000.00") # TODO: Real value
    ws_trade_shares = 10 # TODO: Real Value
    ws_estimated_price = Decimal("150.00") # TODO: Real value
    ws_trade_symbol = "AAPL" # TODO: Real value
    
    if trade_buy:
        ws_required_funds = ws_trade_shares * ws_estimated_price
        if ws_required_funds > ws_available_cash:
            ws_sufficient_flag = 'N'
            ws_reject_reason = 'INSUFFICIENT FUNDS'
    if trade_sell:
        ws_current_shares = check_share_position_C7(ws_trade_symbol)
        if ws_current_shares < ws_trade_shares:
            ws_sufficient_flag = 'N'
            ws_reject_reason = 'INSUFFICIENT SHARES'

def check_share_position_C7(ws_trade_symbol: str) -> Decimal:
    """Checks current share position for a symbol."""
    ws_current_shares = Decimal("0.00")
    return ws_current_shares

def route_order_C7() -> None:
    """Routes a trade order."""
    ws_trade_amount = Decimal("10000.00") # TODO: Real value
    if ws_trade_amount > 100000:
        ws_routing_type = 'ALGO'
    elif ws_trade_amount > 10000:
        ws_routing_type = 'SMART'
    else:
        ws_routing_type = 'DIRECT'
    ws_order_time = "current_date" # TODO: Get current date

def execute_order_C7() -> None:
    """Executes a trade order."""
    order_market = True # TODO: Real value
    order_limit = False # TODO: Real value
    order_stop = False # TODO: Real value
    order_stop_limit = False # TODO: Real value
    if order_market:
        market_order_C7()
    elif order_limit:
        limit_order_C7()
    elif order_stop:
        stop_order_C7()
    elif order_stop_limit:
        stop_limit_order_C7()

def market_order_C7() -> None:
    """Executes a market order."""
    ws_current_market_price = Decimal("150.00") # TODO: Real value
    ws_executed_price = ws_current_market_price
    ws_trade_status = 'FILLED'
    ws_execution_time = "current_date" # TODO: Get current date

def limit_order_C7() -> None:
    """Executes a limit order."""
    trade_buy = True # TODO: Real value
    ws_current_market_price = Decimal("150.00") # TODO: Real value
    ws_limit_price = Decimal("145.00") # TODO: Real value
    if trade_buy:
        if ws_current_market_price <= ws_limit_price:
            ws_executed_price = ws_current_market_price
            ws_trade_status = 'FILLED'
        else:
            ws_trade_status = 'OPEN'
    else:
        if ws_current_market_price >= ws_limit_price:
            ws_executed_price = ws_current_market_price
            ws_trade_status = 'FILLED'
        else:
            ws_trade_status = 'OPEN'

def stop_order_C7() -> None:
    """Executes a stop order."""
    trade_sell = True # TODO: Real value
    ws_current_market_price = Decimal("150.00") # TODO: Real value
    ws_stop_price = Decimal("155.00") # TODO: Real value
    if trade_sell:
        if ws_current_market_price <= ws_stop_price:
            ws_executed_price = ws_current_market_price
            ws_trade_status = 'FILLED'
        else:
            ws_trade_status = 'OPEN'

def stop_limit_order_C7() -> None:
    """Executes a stop-limit order."""
    ws_current_market_price = Decimal("150.00") # TODO: Real value
    ws_stop_price = Decimal("155.00") # TODO: Real value
    if ws_current_market_price <= ws_stop_price:
        limit_order_C7()
    else:
        ws_trade_status = 'OPEN'

def settle_trade_C7() -> None:
    """Settles a trade."""
    ws_trade_status = 'FILLED' # TODO: get the real value
    if ws_trade_status == 'FILLED':
        calculate_costs_C7()
        update_positions_C7()
        update_cash_C7()
        record_trade_C7()

def calculate_costs_C7() -> None:
    """Calculates trade costs."""
    ws_trade_shares = 10 # TODO: real value
    ws_executed_price = Decimal("150.00") # TODO: real value
    trade_buy = True # TODO: real value

    ws_gross_amount = ws_trade_shares * ws_executed_price
    if ws_gross_amount > 100000:
        ws_commission = ws_gross_amount * Decimal("0.0005")
    elif ws_gross_amount > 10000:
        ws_commission = ws_gross_amount * Decimal("0.001")
    else:
        ws_commission = Decimal("4.95")
    ws_fees = ws_gross_amount * Decimal("0.00002")
    if trade_buy:
        ws_net_amount = ws_gross_amount + ws_commission + ws_fees
    else:
        ws_net_amount = ws_gross_amount - ws_commission - ws_fees

    """Updates security positions."""
    trade_buy = True # TODO: real value
    if trade_buy:
        add_to_position_C7()
    else:
        reduce_position_C7()

def calc_auto_premium_C8(ws_driver_age: int, ws_base_premium: Decimal, ws_accidents_3yr: int, ws_violations_3yr: int, ws_annual_premium: Decimal, ws_monthly_premium: Decimal) -> None:
    """Calculates auto premium."""
    if 6 <= ws_driver_age <= 10:
        ws_base_premium += 100
    else:
        ws_base_premium += 50
    if ws_driver_age < 25:
        ws_base_premium *= 1.5
    if ws_accidents_3yr > 0:
        ws_accident_surcharge = ws_accidents_3yr * 200
        ws_base_premium += ws_accident_surcharge
    if ws_violations_3yr > 0:
        ws_violation_surcharge = ws_violations_3yr * 100
        ws_base_premium += ws_violation_surcharge
    ws_annual_premium = ws_base_premium
    ws_monthly_premium = ws_annual_premium / 12

def calc_home_premium_C8(ws_coverage_amount: Decimal, ws_base_premium: Decimal, ws_home_age: int, ws_flood_zone: str, ws_security_system: str, ws_deductible: Decimal, ws_annual_premium: Decimal, ws_monthly_premium: Decimal) -> None:
    """Calculates home premium."""
    ws_base_premium = ws_coverage_amount * Decimal("0.003")
    if 0 <= ws_home_age <= 10:
        ws_base_premium *= Decimal("0.9")
    elif 11 <= ws_home_age <= 25:
        ws_base_premium *= Decimal("1.0")
    elif 26 <= ws_home_age <= 50:
        ws_base_premium *= Decimal("1.2")
    else:
        ws_base_premium *= Decimal("1.5")
    if ws_flood_zone == 'Y':
        ws_base_premium *= Decimal("1.5")
    if ws_security_system == 'Y':
        ws_base_premium *= Decimal("0.9")
    ws_deductible_credit = ws_deductible / 1000 * 50
    ws_base_premium -= ws_deductible_credit
    if ws_base_premium < 200:
        ws_base_premium = Decimal("200")
    ws_annual_premium = ws_base_premium
    ws_monthly_premium = ws_annual_premium / 12

def calc_health_premium_C8(ws_insured_age: int, ws_base_premium: Decimal, ws_plan_type: str, ws_family_plan: str, ws_monthly_premium: Decimal, ws_annual_premium: Decimal) -> None:
    """Calculates health premium."""
    ws_base_premium = Decimal("300")
    if 0 <= ws_insured_age <= 18:
        ws_base_premium *= Decimal("0.5")
    elif 19 <= ws_insured_age <= 30:
        ws_base_premium *= Decimal("1.0")
    elif 31 <= ws_insured_age <= 40:
        ws_base_premium *= Decimal("1.3")
    elif 41 <= ws_insured_age <= 50:
        ws_base_premium *= Decimal("1.6")
    elif 51 <= ws_insured_age <= 60:
        ws_base_premium *= Decimal("2.0")
    else:
        ws_base_premium *= Decimal("2.8")
    if ws_plan_type == 'BRONZE':
        ws_base_premium *= Decimal("0.8")
    elif ws_plan_type == 'SILVER':
        ws_base_premium *= Decimal("1.0")
    elif ws_plan_type == 'GOLD':
        ws_base_premium *= Decimal("1.3")
    elif ws_plan_type == 'PLATINUM':
        ws_base_premium *= Decimal("1.6")
    if ws_family_plan == 'Y':
        ws_base_premium *= Decimal("2.5")
    ws_monthly_premium = ws_base_premium
    ws_annual_premium = ws_monthly_premium * 12

def underwriting_C8() -> None:
    """Performs underwriting."""
    evaluate_risk_factors_C8()
    check_medical_history_C8()
    verify_information_C8()
    determine_decision_C8()

def evaluate_risk_factors_C8(policy_life: bool, ws_bmi: int, ws_smoker_flag: str, ws_hazardous_occupation: str, policy_auto: bool, ws_driver_age: int, ws_accidents_3yr: int, ws_risk_points: int) -> None:
    """Evaluates risk factors."""
    ws_risk_points = 0
    if policy_life:
        if ws_bmi > 30:
            ws_risk_points += 10
        if ws_smoker_flag == 'Y':
            ws_risk_points += 25
        if ws_hazardous_occupation == 'Y':
            ws_risk_points += 15
    if policy_auto:
        if ws_driver_age < 21:
            ws_risk_points += 20
        if ws_accidents_3yr > 1:
            ws_risk_points += 15

def check_medical_history_C8(ws_chronic_conditions: int, ws_risk_points: int, ws_recent_hospitalization: str, ws_prescription_count: int, ws_condition_points: int) -> None:
    """Checks medical history."""
    if ws_chronic_conditions > 0:
        ws_condition_points = ws_chronic_conditions * 5
        ws_risk_points += ws_condition_points
    if ws_recent_hospitalization == 'Y':
        ws_risk_points += 10
    if ws_prescription_count > 5:
        ws_risk_points += 5

def verify_information_C8() -> None:
    """Verifies information."""
    check_fraud_indicators_C8()
    validate_documents_C8()

def check_fraud_indicators_C8(ws_recent_claims: int, ws_risk_points: int, ws_fraud_flag: str, ws_address_mismatch: str) -> None:
    """Checks fraud indicators."""
    if ws_recent_claims > 3:
        ws_risk_points += 20
        ws_fraud_flag = 'Y'
    if ws_address_mismatch == 'Y':
        ws_risk_points += 10

def validate_documents_C8(ws_doc_missing: str, ws_uw_status: str) -> None:
    """Validates documents."""
    if ws_doc_missing == 'Y':
        ws_uw_status = 'PENDING'
    else:
        ws_uw_status = 'COMPLETE'

def determine_decision_C8(ws_risk_points: int, ws_uw_decision: str, ws_annual_premium: Decimal) -> None:
    """Determines decision."""
    if ws_risk_points > 50:
        ws_uw_decision = 'DECLINE'
    elif ws_risk_points > 30:
        ws_uw_decision = 'SUBSTANDARD'
        ws_annual_premium *= Decimal("1.5")
    elif ws_risk_points > 15:
        ws_uw_decision = 'STANDARD'
    else:
        ws_uw_decision = 'PREFERRED'
        ws_annual_premium *= Decimal("0.9")

def issue_policy_C8(ws_uw_decision: str) -> None:
    """Issues policy."""
    if ws_uw_decision != 'DECLINE':
        generate_policy_number_C8()
        create_policy_record_C8()
        set_beneficiaries_C8()
        send_policy_docs_C8()
    else:
        send_decline_letter_C8()

def generate_policy_number_C8(ws_date_part: str, ws_policy_type: str, ws_type_part: str, ws_random_part: int, ws_policy_number: str) -> None:
    """Generates policy number."""
    import datetime
    ws_date_part = datetime.datetime.now().strftime("%Y%m%d")
    ws_type_part = ws_policy_type
    import random
    ws_random_part = int(random.random() * 99999)
    ws_policy_number = f"{ws_type_part}{ws_date_part}{ws_random_part}"

def create_policy_record_C8(ws_policy_number: str, ws_policy_type: str, ws_coverage_amount: Decimal, ws_annual_premium: Decimal, ws_effective_date: str, ws_expiration_date: str) -> None:
    """Creates policy record."""
    @dataclass
    class PolicyRecord:
        policy_rec_number: str = ""
        policy_rec_type: str = ""
        policy_rec_coverage: Decimal = Decimal("0.00")
        policy_rec_premium: Decimal = Decimal("0.00")
        policy_rec_eff_date: str = ""
        policy_rec_exp_date: str = ""
        policy_rec_status: str = ""

    ws_policy_record = PolicyRecord()
    ws_policy_record.policy_rec_number = ws_policy_number
    ws_policy_record.policy_rec_type = ws_policy_type
    ws_policy_record.policy_rec_coverage = ws_coverage_amount
    ws_policy_record.policy_rec_premium = ws_annual_premium
    ws_policy_record.policy_rec_eff_date = ws_effective_date
    ws_policy_record.policy_rec_exp_date = ws_expiration_date
    ws_policy_record.policy_rec_status = 'A'

    print(f"Writing Policy Record: {ws_policy_record}")
    #In real application write to file/DB

def set_beneficiaries_C8(ws_policy_number: str, benef_name: list[str], benef_relation: list[str], benef_pct: list[int]) -> None:
    """Sets beneficiaries."""
    @dataclass
    class BeneficiaryRecord:
        benef_rec_policy: str = ""
        benef_rec_name: str = ""
        benef_rec_relation: str = ""
        benef_rec_pct: int = 0

    for ws_benef_idx in range(5):
        if benef_name[ws_benef_idx] != "":
            ws_beneficiary_rec = BeneficiaryRecord()
            ws_beneficiary_rec.benef_rec_policy = ws_policy_number
            ws_beneficiary_rec.benef_rec_name = benef_name[ws_benef_idx]
            ws_beneficiary_rec.benef_rec_relation = benef_relation[ws_benef_idx]
            ws_beneficiary_rec.benef_rec_pct = benef_pct[ws_benef_idx]

            print(f"Writing Beneficiary Record: {ws_beneficiary_rec}")
            #In real application write to file/DB

def generate_claim_number_C8(ws_date_part: str, ws_random_part: int, ws_claim_number: str) -> None:
    """Generates claim number."""

    import random
    ws_random_part = int(random.random() * 99999)
    ws_claim_number = f"CLM{ws_date_part}{ws_random_part}"

def validate_claim_C8() -> None:
    """Validates claim."""
    check_policy_status_C8()
    check_coverage_C8()
    check_deductible_C8()

def check_policy_status_C8(ws_policy_status: str, ws_claim_status: str, ws_claim_deny_reason: str) -> None:
    """Checks policy status."""
    if ws_policy_status != 'A':
        ws_claim_status = 'DENIED'
        ws_claim_deny_reason = 'POLICY NOT ACTIVE'

def check_coverage_C8(ws_claim_type: str, ws_covered_perils: str, ws_claim_status: str, ws_claim_deny_reason: str) -> None:
    """Checks coverage."""
    if ws_claim_type != ws_covered_perils:
        ws_claim_status = 'DENIED'
        ws_claim_deny_reason = 'NOT COVERED PERIL'

def check_deductible_C8(ws_claim_amount: Decimal, ws_deductible: Decimal, ws_claim_status: str, ws_claim_deny_reason: str) -> None:
    """Checks deductible."""
    if ws_claim_amount <= ws_deductible:
        ws_claim_status = 'DENIED'
        ws_claim_deny_reason = 'BELOW DEDUCTIBLE'

def investigate_claim_C8(ws_claim_amount: Decimal, ws_claim_status: str, ws_coverage_amount: Decimal) -> None:
    """Investigates claim."""
    if ws_claim_amount > 10000:
        ws_claim_status = 'INVESTIGATION'
        assign_adjuster_C8()
    fraud_check_C8()

def assign_adjuster_C8(ws_adjuster_id: str, ws_notes: str) -> None:
    """Assigns adjuster."""
    ws_adjuster_id = 'ADJ001'
    ws_notes = 'Assigned for investigation'

def fraud_check_C8(ws_recent_claims: int, ws_fraud_review: str, ws_claim_amount: Decimal, ws_coverage_amount: Decimal) -> None:
    """Checks for fraud."""
    if ws_recent_claims > 2:
        ws_fraud_review = 'Y'
    if ws_claim_amount > ws_coverage_amount * Decimal("0.8"):
        ws_fraud_review = 'Y'

def adjudicate_claim_C8(ws_claim_status: str, ws_claim_amount: Decimal, ws_deductible: Decimal, ws_approved_amount: Decimal, ws_coverage_amount: Decimal) -> None:
    """Adjudicates claim."""
    if ws_claim_status != 'DENIED':
        ws_approved_amount = ws_claim_amount - ws_deductible
        if ws_approved_amount > ws_coverage_amount:
            ws_approved_amount = ws_coverage_amount
        ws_claim_status = 'APPROVED'

def process_payment_C8(ws_claim_status: str) -> None:
    """Processes payment."""
    if ws_claim_status == 'APPROVED':
        issue_payment_C8()
        update_claim_record_C8()

    ws_payment_record.pay_rec_amount = ws_approved_amount
    import datetime
    ws_payment_record.pay_rec_date = datetime.datetime.now().strftime("%Y%m%d")
    ws_payment_record.pay_rec_method = 'CHECK'

    print(f"Writing Payment Record: {ws_payment_record}")
    #In real application write to file/DB

def calculate_gross_pay_C8(ws_pay_type: str) -> None:
    """Calculates gross pay."""
    if ws_pay_type == 'SALARY':
        calc_salary_pay_C8()
    elif ws_pay_type == 'HOURLY':
        calc_hourly_pay_C8()
    elif ws_pay_type == 'COMMISSION':
        calc_commission_pay_C8()

def calc_salary_pay_C8(ws_annual_salary: Decimal, ws_pay_periods: int, ws_gross_pay: Decimal) -> None:
    """Calculates salary pay."""
    ws_gross_pay = ws_annual_salary / ws_pay_periods

def calc_hourly_pay_C8(ws_hours_worked: int, ws_hourly_rate: Decimal, ws_regular_pay: Decimal, ws_overtime_pay: Decimal, ws_ot_hours: int, ws_gross_pay: Decimal) -> None:
    """Calculates hourly pay."""
    if ws_hours_worked <= 40:
        ws_regular_pay = ws_hours_worked * ws_hourly_rate
        ws_overtime_pay = Decimal("0.00")
    else:
        ws_regular_pay = 40 * ws_hourly_rate
        ws_ot_hours = ws_hours_worked - 40
        ws_overtime_pay = ws_ot_hours * ws_hourly_rate * Decimal("1.5")
    ws_gross_pay = ws_regular_pay + ws_overtime_pay

def calc_commission_pay_C8(ws_base_salary: Decimal, ws_pay_periods: int, ws_base_pay: Decimal, ws_commission_pay: Decimal, ws_sales_amount: Decimal, ws_commission_rate: Decimal, ws_gross_pay: Decimal) -> None:
    """Calculates commission pay."""
    ws_base_pay = ws_base_salary / ws_pay_periods
    ws_commission_pay = ws_sales_amount * ws_commission_rate
    ws_gross_pay = ws_base_pay + ws_commission_pay

def calculate_taxes_C8() -> None:
    """Calculates taxes."""
    calc_federal_tax_C8()
    calc_state_tax_C8()
    calc_local_tax_C8()
    calc_fica_C8()

def calc_federal_tax_C8(ws_gross_pay: Decimal, ws_pay_periods: int, ws_annualized_gross: Decimal, ws_exemptions: int, ws_allowance_amount: Decimal, ws_taxable_income: Decimal, ws_annual_tax: Decimal, ws_federal_tax: Decimal) -> None:
    """Calculates federal tax."""
    ws_annualized_gross = ws_gross_pay * ws_pay_periods
    ws_allowance_amount = ws_exemptions * 4300
    ws_taxable_income = ws_annualized_gross - ws_allowance_amount
    if ws_taxable_income < 0:
        ws_taxable_income = Decimal("0.00")
    apply_tax_brackets_C8(ws_taxable_income, ws_annual_tax)
    ws_federal_tax = ws_annual_tax / ws_pay_periods

def apply_tax_brackets_C8(ws_taxable_income: Decimal, ws_annual_tax: Decimal) -> None:
    """Applies tax brackets."""
    ws_annual_tax = Decimal("0.00")
    @dataclass
    class StatusFlags:
        status_single: bool = False
        status_married_joint: bool = False
    status_flags = StatusFlags()
    if status_flags.status_single:
        single_brackets_C8(ws_taxable_income, ws_annual_tax)
    elif status_flags.status_married_joint:
        married_brackets_C8(ws_taxable_income, ws_annual_tax)

def single_brackets_C8(ws_taxable_income: Decimal, ws_annual_tax: Decimal) -> None:
    """Applies single tax brackets."""
    if ws_taxable_income <= 10275:
        ws_annual_tax = ws_taxable_income * Decimal("0.10")
    elif ws_taxable_income <= 41775:
        ws_annual_tax = Decimal("1027.50") + (ws_taxable_income - 10275) * Decimal("0.12")
    elif ws_taxable_income <= 89075:
        ws_annual_tax = Decimal("4807.50") + (ws_taxable_income - 41775) * Decimal("0.22")
    elif ws_taxable_income <= 170050:
        ws_annual_tax = Decimal("15213.50") + (ws_taxable_income - 89075) * Decimal("0.24")
    elif ws_taxable_income <= 215950:
        ws_annual_tax = Decimal("34647.50") + (ws_taxable_income - 170050) * Decimal("0.32")
    elif ws_taxable_income <= 539900:
        ws_annual_tax = Decimal("49335.50") + (ws_taxable_income - 215950) * Decimal("0.35")
    else:
        ws_annual_tax = Decimal("162718.00") + (ws_taxable_income - 539900) * Decimal("0.37")

def married_brackets_C8(ws_taxable_income: Decimal, ws_annual_tax: Decimal) -> None:
    """Applies married tax brackets."""
    if ws_taxable_income <= 20550:
        ws_annual_tax = ws_taxable_income * Decimal("0.10")
    elif ws_taxable_income <= 83550:
        ws_annual_tax = Decimal("2055.00") + (ws_taxable_income - 20550) * Decimal("0.12")
    elif ws_taxable_income <= 178150:
        ws_annual_tax = Decimal("9615.00") + (ws_taxable_income - 83550) * Decimal("0.22")
    elif ws_taxable_income <= 340100:
        ws_annual_tax = Decimal("30427.00") + (ws_taxable_income - 178150) * Decimal("0.24")
    elif ws_taxable_income <= 431900:
        ws_annual_tax = Decimal("69295.00") + (ws_taxable_income - 340100) * Decimal("0.32")
    elif ws_taxable_income <= 647850:
        ws_annual_tax = Decimal("98671.00") + (ws_taxable_income - 431900) * Decimal("0.35")
    else:
        ws_annual_tax = Decimal("174253.50") + (ws_taxable_income - 647850) * Decimal("0.37")

def calc_state_tax_C8(ws_state_code: str, ws_gross_pay: Decimal, ws_state_tax: Decimal) -> None:
    """Calculates state tax."""
    if ws_state_code == 'CA':
        ws_state_tax = ws_gross_pay * Decimal("0.0725")
    elif ws_state_code == 'NY':
        ws_state_tax = ws_gross_pay * Decimal("0.0685")
    elif ws_state_code == 'TX':
        ws_state_tax = Decimal("0.00")
    elif ws_state_code == 'FL':
        ws_state_tax = Decimal("0.00")
    else:
        ws_state_tax = ws_gross_pay * Decimal("0.05")

def calc_local_tax_C8(ws_local_tax_rate: Decimal, ws_gross_pay: Decimal, ws_local_tax: Decimal) -> None:
    """Calculates local tax."""
    if ws_local_tax_rate > 0:
        ws_local_tax = ws_gross_pay * ws_local_tax_rate
    else:
        ws_local_tax = Decimal("0.00")

def calc_fica_C8(ws_ytd_gross: Decimal, ws_gross_pay: Decimal, ws_remaining_cap: Decimal, ws_fica_ss: Decimal, ws_fica_medicare: Decimal, ws_additional_medicare: Decimal) -> None:
    """Calculates FICA."""
    if ws_ytd_gross < 160200:
        ws_remaining_cap = 160200 - ws_ytd_gross
        if ws_gross_pay <= ws_remaining_cap:
            ws_fica_ss = ws_gross_pay * Decimal("0.062")
        else:
            ws_fica_ss = ws_remaining_cap * Decimal("0.062")
    else:
        ws_fica_ss = Decimal("0.00")
    ws_fica_medicare = ws_gross_pay * Decimal("0.0145")
    if ws_ytd_gross > 200000:
        ws_additional_medicare = ws_gross_pay * Decimal("0.009")
        ws_fica_medicare += ws_additional_medicare

def calculate_deductions_C8() -> None:
    """Calculates deductions."""
    calc_pre_tax_deductions_C8()
    calc_post_tax_deductions_C8()

def calc_pre_tax_deductions_C8(ws_401k_pct: Decimal, ws_gross_pay: Decimal, ws_401k_contrib: Decimal, ws_ytd_401k: Decimal, ws_health_ins_deduct: Decimal, ws_health_ins: Decimal, ws_dental_ins_deduct: Decimal, ws_dental_ins: Decimal, ws_vision_ins_deduct: Decimal, ws_vision_ins: Decimal, ws_hsa_deduct: Decimal, ws_hsa_contrib: Decimal, ws_fsa_deduct: Decimal, ws_fsa_contrib: Decimal) -> None:
    """Calculates pre-tax deductions."""
    if ws_401k_pct > 0:
        ws_401k_contrib = ws_gross_pay * ws_401k_pct / 100
        if ws_ytd_401k + ws_401k_contrib > 22500:
            ws_401k_contrib = 22500 - ws_ytd_401k
            if ws_401k_contrib < 0:
                ws_401k_contrib = Decimal("0.00")
    ws_health_ins = ws_health_ins_deduct
    ws_dental_ins = ws_dental_ins_deduct
    ws_vision_ins = ws_vision_ins_deduct
    ws_hsa_contrib = ws_hsa_deduct
    ws_fsa_contrib = ws_fsa_deduct

def calc_post_tax_deductions_C8(ws_life_ins_deduct: Decimal, ws_life_ins: Decimal, ws_disability_deduct: Decimal, ws_disability_ins: Decimal, ws_union_dues_amt: Decimal, ws_union_dues: Decimal, ws_garnishment_amt: Decimal, ws_garnishment: Decimal) -> None:
    """Calculates post-tax deductions."""
    ws_life_ins = ws_life_ins_deduct
    ws_disability_ins = ws_disability_deduct
    ws_union_dues = ws_union_dues_amt
    ws_garnishment = ws_garnishment_amt

def calculate_net_pay_C8(ws_gross_pay: Decimal, ws_federal_tax: Decimal, ws_state_tax: Decimal, ws_local_tax: Decimal, ws_fica_ss: Decimal, ws_fica_medicare: Decimal, ws_health_ins: Decimal, ws_dental_ins: Decimal, ws_vision_ins: Decimal, ws_401k_contrib: Decimal, ws_hsa_contrib: Decimal, ws_fsa_contrib: Decimal, ws_life_ins: Decimal, ws_disability_ins: Decimal, ws_union_dues: Decimal, ws_garnishment: Decimal, ws_other_deduct: Decimal, ws_total_deductions: Decimal, ws_net_pay: Decimal) -> None:
    """Calculates net pay."""
    ws_total_deductions = (ws_federal_tax + ws_state_tax + ws_local_tax + 0  # TODO
                              ws_fica_ss + ws_fica_medicare + 0  # TODO
                              ws_health_ins + ws_dental_ins + ws_vision_ins + 0  # TODO
                              ws_401k_contrib + ws_hsa_contrib + ws_fsa_contrib + 0  # TODO
                              ws_life_ins + ws_disability_ins + 0  # TODO
                              ws_union_dues + ws_garnishment + ws_other_deduct)
    ws_net_pay = ws_gross_pay - ws_total_deductions
    update_ytd_totals_C8(ws_gross_pay, ws_federal_tax, ws_state_tax, ws_fica_ss, ws_fica_medicare, ws_net_pay, ws_401k_contrib)

def update_ytd_totals_C8(ws_gross_pay: Decimal, ws_federal_tax: Decimal, ws_state_tax: Decimal, ws_fica_ss: Decimal, ws_fica_medicare: Decimal, ws_net_pay: Decimal, ws_401k_contrib: Decimal) -> None:
    """Updates year-to-date totals."""
    @dataclass

        ws_ytd_gross: Decimal = Decimal("0.00")
        ws_ytd_fed_tax: Decimal = Decimal("0.00")
        ws_ytd_state_tax: Decimal = Decimal("0.00")
        ws_ytd_fica: Decimal = Decimal("0.00")
        ws_ytd_net: Decimal = Decimal("0.00")
        ws_ytd_401k:

import decimal


def calculate_next_run_date_C10(ws_last_run_date: str, run_frequency: str) -> None:
    """Calculates the next run date."""
    ws_next_run_date: int
    if run_frequency == 'DAILY':
        ws_next_run_date = int(ws_last_run_date) + 1
    elif run_frequency == 'WEEKLY':
        ws_next_run_date = int(ws_last_run_date) + 7
    elif run_frequency == 'MONTHLY':
        ws_next_run_date = int(ws_last_run_date) + 30
    elif run_frequency == 'QUARTERLY':
        ws_next_run_date = int(ws_last_run_date) + 90
    elif run_frequency == 'YEARLY':
        ws_next_run_date = int(ws_last_run_date) + 365
    else:
        pass
