def collect_metrics_C10() -> None:
    """Collects various metrics."""
    collect_transaction_metrics_C10()
    collect_customer_metrics_C10()
    collect_performance_metrics_C10()


def collect_transaction_metrics_C10() -> None:
    """Collects transaction-related metrics."""
    global ws_total_trans_amount, ws_total_trans_count, ws_avg_trans_amount, ws_eof_flag
    global transaction_file, ws_trans_rec, trans_amount
    ws_total_trans_amount = decimal.Decimal("0")
    ws_total_trans_count = 0
    ws_avg_trans_amount = decimal.Decimal("0")
    ws_eof_flag = 'N'

    while ws_eof_flag != 'Y':
        try:
            ws_trans_rec = transaction_file.readline().strip()
            if not ws_trans_rec:
                ws_eof_flag = 'Y'
            else:
                ws_total_trans_count += 1
                trans_amount = decimal.Decimal("100")
                ws_total_trans_amount += trans_amount
        except Exception as e:
            ws_eof_flag = 'Y'

    if ws_total_trans_count > 0:
        ws_avg_trans_amount = ws_total_trans_amount / ws_total_trans_count
    ws_eof_flag = 'N'


def collect_customer_metrics_C10() -> None:
    """Collects customer-related metrics."""
    global ws_active_customers, ws_new_customers, ws_churned_customers, ws_eof_flag
    global customer_file, ws_cust_rec, cust_status, cust_open_date, cust_close_date, ws_period_start

    ws_active_customers = 0
    ws_new_customers = 0
    ws_churned_customers = 0
    ws_eof_flag = 'N'

    while ws_eof_flag != 'Y':
        try:
            ws_cust_rec = customer_file.readline().strip()
            if not ws_cust_rec:
                ws_eof_flag = 'Y'
            else:
                cust_status = 'A'
                cust_open_date = "20240101"
                cust_close_date = "20240101"
                ws_period_start = "20240101"

                if cust_status == 'A':
                    ws_active_customers += 1
                if cust_open_date >= ws_period_start:
                    ws_new_customers += 1
                if cust_close_date >= ws_period_start:
                    ws_churned_customers += 1
        except Exception as e:
            ws_eof_flag = 'Y'
    ws_eof_flag = 'N'


def collect_performance_metrics_C10() -> None:
    """Collects performance-related metrics."""
    global ws_response_time_total, ws_response_count, ws_avg_response_time, ws_eof_flag
    global perf_log_file, ws_perf_rec, perf_response_time
    ws_response_time_total = decimal.Decimal("0")
    ws_response_count = 0
    ws_eof_flag = 'N'

    while ws_eof_flag != 'Y':
        try:
            ws_perf_rec = perf_log_file.readline().strip()
            if not ws_perf_rec:
                ws_eof_flag = 'Y'
            else:
                perf_response_time = decimal.Decimal("10")
                ws_response_time_total += perf_response_time
                ws_response_count += 1
        except Exception as e:
            ws_eof_flag = 'Y'

    if ws_response_count > 0:
        ws_avg_response_time = ws_response_time_total / ws_response_count
    ws_eof_flag = 'N'


def aggregate_data_C10() -> None:
    """Aggregates collected data."""
    daily_aggregation_C10()
    weekly_aggregation_C10()
    monthly_aggregation_C10()


def daily_aggregation_C10() -> None:
    """Performs daily data aggregation."""
    global ws_daily_summary, ws_process_date, ws_total_trans_count, ws_total_trans_amount, ws_total_deposits, ws_total_withdrawals
    global daily_date, daily_trans_count, daily_trans_amount, daily_deposits, daily_withdrawals
    global daily_summary_record
    ws_daily_summary = {}
    ws_process_date = "20240101"
    ws_total_trans_count = 100
    ws_total_trans_amount = decimal.Decimal("1000.00")
    ws_total_deposits = decimal.Decimal("500.00")
    ws_total_withdrawals = decimal.Decimal("500.00")

    daily_date = ws_process_date
    daily_trans_count = ws_total_trans_count
    daily_trans_amount = ws_total_trans_amount
    daily_deposits = ws_total_deposits
    daily_withdrawals = ws_total_withdrawals

    daily_summary_record = ws_daily_summary


def weekly_aggregation_C10() -> None:
    """Performs weekly data aggregation."""
    global ws_day_of_week, ws_week_number
    global weekly_week, weekly_summary_record, ws_weekly_summary
    ws_day_of_week = 7
    ws_week_number = 1

    if ws_day_of_week == 7:
        ws_weekly_summary = {}
        weekly_week = ws_week_number
        sum_week_data_C10()
        weekly_summary_record = ws_weekly_summary


def sum_week_data_C10() -> None:
    """Sums data for the week."""
    global weekly_trans_count, weekly_trans_amount, daily_trans_count, daily_trans_amount
    weekly_trans_count = 0
    weekly_trans_amount = decimal.Decimal("0")
    daily_trans_count = 10
    daily_trans_amount = decimal.Decimal("100")

    for _ in range(7):
        weekly_trans_count += daily_trans_count
        weekly_trans_amount += daily_trans_amount


def monthly_aggregation_C10() -> None:
    """Performs monthly data aggregation."""
    global ws_end_of_month, ws_curr_month, ws_curr_year
    global monthly_month, monthly_year, monthly_summary_record, ws_monthly_summary

    ws_end_of_month = 'Y'
    ws_curr_month = 1
    ws_curr_year = 2024

    if ws_end_of_month == 'Y':
        ws_monthly_summary = {}
        monthly_month = ws_curr_month
        monthly_year = ws_curr_year
        sum_month_data_C10()
        monthly_summary_record = ws_monthly_summary


def sum_month_data_C10() -> None:
    """Sums data for the month."""
    global monthly_trans_count, monthly_trans_amount, monthly_new_accounts, monthly_closed_accounts, ws_eof_flag
    global daily_summary_file, ws_daily_sum_rec, daily_month, ws_curr_month
    monthly_trans_count = 0
    monthly_trans_amount = decimal.Decimal("0")
    monthly_new_accounts = 0
    monthly_closed_accounts = 0
    ws_eof_flag = 'N'

    while ws_eof_flag != 'Y':
        try:
            ws_daily_sum_rec = daily_summary_file.readline().strip()
            if not ws_daily_sum_rec:
                ws_eof_flag = 'Y'
            else:
                daily_month = 1
                if daily_month == ws_curr_month:
                    daily_trans_count = 10
                    daily_trans_amount = decimal.Decimal("100")
                    monthly_trans_count += daily_trans_count
                    monthly_trans_amount += daily_trans_amount
        except Exception as e:
            ws_eof_flag = 'Y'
    ws_eof_flag = 'N'


def calculate_kpi_C10() -> None:
    """Calculates Key Performance Indicators (KPIs)."""
    calc_financial_kpi_C10()
    calc_operational_kpi_C10()
    calc_customer_kpi_C10()


def calc_financial_kpi_C10() -> None:
    """Calculates financial KPIs."""
    global ws_total_assets, ws_net_income, ws_roa, ws_total_equity, ws_roe, ws_interest_expense, ws_interest_income, ws_nim, ws_earning_assets

    ws_total_assets = decimal.Decimal("1000000.00")
    ws_net_income = decimal.Decimal("100000.00")
    ws_total_equity = decimal.Decimal("500000.00")
    ws_interest_expense = decimal.Decimal("10000.00")
    ws_interest_income = decimal.Decimal("50000.00")
    ws_earning_assets = decimal.Decimal("800000.00")
    ws_roa = decimal.Decimal("0")
    ws_roe = decimal.Decimal("0")
    ws_nim = decimal.Decimal("0")

    if ws_total_assets > 0:
        ws_roa = (ws_net_income / ws_total_assets) * 100
    if ws_total_equity > 0:
        ws_roe = (ws_net_income / ws_total_equity) * 100
    if ws_interest_expense > 0:
        ws_nim = ((ws_interest_income - ws_interest_expense) / ws_earning_assets) * 100


def calc_operational_kpi_C10() -> None:
    """Calculates operational KPIs."""
    global ws_total_trans_count, ws_error_count, ws_error_rate, ws_sla_compliance, ws_within_sla_count, ws_total_cases, ws_first_call_resolution, ws_fcr_count, ws_total_calls

    ws_total_trans_count = 1000
    ws_error_count = 10
    ws_within_sla_count = 95
    ws_total_cases = 100
    ws_fcr_count = 80
    ws_total_calls = 100
    ws_error_rate = decimal.Decimal("0")
    ws_sla_compliance = decimal.Decimal("0")
    ws_first_call_resolution = decimal.Decimal("0")

    if ws_total_trans_count > 0:
        ws_error_rate = (ws_error_count / ws_total_trans_count) * 100
    ws_sla_compliance = (ws_within_sla_count / ws_total_cases) * 100
    ws_first_call_resolution = (ws_fcr_count / ws_total_calls) * 100


def calc_customer_kpi_C10() -> None:
    """Calculates customer KPIs."""
    global ws_active_customers, ws_churned_customers, ws_churn_rate, ws_acquisition_cost, ws_marketing_spend, ws_new_customers, ws_lifetime_value, ws_avg_revenue_per_customer, ws_avg_customer_tenure

    ws_active_customers = 1000
    ws_churned_customers = 100
    ws_marketing_spend = decimal.Decimal("10000.00")
    ws_new_customers = 200
    ws_avg_revenue_per_customer = decimal.Decimal("500.00")
    ws_avg_customer_tenure = 5
    ws_churn_rate = decimal.Decimal("0")
    ws_acquisition_cost = decimal.Decimal("0")
    ws_lifetime_value = decimal.Decimal("0")

    if ws_active_customers > 0:
        ws_churn_rate = (ws_churned_customers / ws_active_customers) * 100
    ws_acquisition_cost = ws_marketing_spend / ws_new_customers
    ws_lifetime_value = ws_avg_revenue_per_customer * ws_avg_customer_tenure


def generate_dashboard_C10() -> None:
    """Generates dashboards."""
    create_executive_dashboard_C10()
    create_operations_dashboard_C10()
    create_risk_dashboard_C10()


def create_executive_dashboard_C10() -> None:
    """Creates an executive dashboard."""
    global dash_title, dash_revenue, dash_net_income, dash_roa, dash_roe, dash_customers, dashboard_record, ws_exec_dashboard
    global ws_total_revenue, ws_net_income, ws_roa, ws_roe, ws_active_customers

    ws_total_revenue = decimal.Decimal("5000000.00")
    ws_net_income = decimal.Decimal("500000.00")
    ws_roa = decimal.Decimal("5.00")
    ws_roe = decimal.Decimal("10.00")
    ws_active_customers = 1000

    ws_exec_dashboard = {}
    dash_title = 'EXECUTIVE DASHBOARD'
    dash_revenue = ws_total_revenue
    dash_net_income = ws_net_income
    dash_roa = ws_roa
    dash_roe = ws_roe
    dash_customers = ws_active_customers
    dashboard_record = ws_exec_dashboard


def create_operations_dashboard_C10() -> None:

    global ws_total_trans_count, ws_avg_response_time, ws_error_rate, ws_sla_compliance

    ws_total_trans_count = 10000
    ws_avg_response_time = decimal.Decimal("0.5")
    ws_error_rate = decimal.Decimal("0.1")
    ws_sla_compliance = decimal.Decimal("99.9")

    ws_ops_dashboard = {}

def create_risk_dashboard_C10() -> None:

    global ws_fraud_score, ws_npl_ratio, ws_capital_ratio, ws_liquidity_ratio

    ws_fraud_score = decimal.Decimal("0.05")

def export_data_C10() -> None:
    """Exports data in various formats."""
    export_csv_C10()
    export_xml_C10()
    export_json_C10()


def export_csv_C10() -> None:
    """Exports data to CSV format."""
    global csv_export_file, ws_csv_header, csv_record, ws_eof_flag, daily_summary_file, ws_daily_sum_rec, daily_date, daily_trans_count, daily_trans_amount, daily_deposits, daily_withdrawals, ws_csv_line
    csv_export_file = open("output.csv", "w")
    ws_csv_header = 'Date,TransCount,TransAmount,Deposits,Withdrawals'
    csv_record = ws_csv_header
    csv_export_file.write(csv_record + "
")
    ws_eof_flag = 'N'

    while ws_eof_flag != 'Y':
        try:
            ws_daily_sum_rec = daily_summary_file.readline().strip()
            if not ws_daily_sum_rec:
                ws_eof_flag = 'Y'
            else:
                daily_date = "20240101"
                daily_trans_count = 10
                daily_trans_amount = decimal.Decimal("100")
                daily_deposits = decimal.Decimal("50")
                daily_withdrawals = decimal.Decimal("50")
                ws_csv_line = f"{daily_date},{daily_trans_count},{daily_trans_amount},{daily_deposits},{daily_withdrawals}"
                csv_record = ws_csv_line
                csv_export_file.write(csv_record + "
")
        except Exception as e:
            ws_eof_flag = 'Y'

    csv_export_file.close()
    ws_eof_flag = 'N'


def export_xml_C10() -> None:
    """Exports data to XML format."""
    global xml_export_file, ws_xml_line, xml_record
    xml_export_file = open("output.xml", "w")
    ws_xml_line = '<?xml version="1.0"?>'
    xml_record = ws_xml_line
    xml_export_file.write(xml_record + "
")
    ws_xml_line = '<DailySummaries>'
    xml_record = ws_xml_line
    xml_export_file.write(xml_record + "
")
    write_xml_records_C10()
    ws_xml_line = '</DailySummaries>'
    xml_record = ws_xml_line
    xml_export_file.write(xml_record + "
")
    xml_export_file.close()


def write_xml_records_C10() -> None:
    """Writes XML records."""
    global ws_eof_flag, daily_summary_file, ws_daily_sum_rec
    ws_eof_flag = 'N'

    while ws_eof_flag != 'Y':
        try:
            ws_daily_sum_rec = daily_summary_file.readline().strip()
            if not ws_daily_sum_rec:
                ws_eof_flag = 'Y'
            else:
                format_xml_record_C10()
        except Exception as e:
            ws_eof_flag = 'Y'

    ws_eof_flag = 'N'


def format_xml_record_C10() -> None:
    """Formats an XML record."""
    global ws_xml_line, xml_record, daily_date, daily_trans_count
    ws_xml_line = '<Summary>'
    xml_record = ws_xml_line
    xml_export_file.write(xml_record + "
")
    daily_date = "20240101"
    daily_trans_count = 10
    ws_xml_line = f'<Date>{daily_date}</Date>'
    xml_record = ws_xml_line
    xml_export_file.write(xml_record + "
")
    ws_xml_line = f'<TransCount>{daily_trans_count}</TransCount>'
    xml_record = ws_xml_line
    xml_export_file.write(xml_record + "
")
    ws_xml_line = '</Summary>'
    xml_record = ws_xml_line
    xml_export_file.write(xml_record + "
")


def export_json_C10() -> None:
    """Exports data to JSON format."""
    global json_export_file, ws_json_line, json_record
    json_export_file = open("output.json", "w")
    ws_json_line = '{"dailySummaries":['
    json_record = ws_json_line
    json_export_file.write(json_record + "
")
    write_json_records_C10()
    ws_json_line = ']}'
    json_record = ws_json_line
    json_export_file.write(json_record + "
")
    json_export_file.close()


def write_json_records_C10() -> None:
    """Writes JSON records."""
    global ws_eof_flag, daily_summary_file, ws_daily_sum_rec, ws_first_record
    ws_first_record = 'N'
    ws_eof_flag = 'N'

    while ws_eof_flag != 'Y':
        try:
            ws_daily_sum_rec = daily_summary_file.readline().strip()
            if not ws_daily_sum_rec:
                ws_eof_flag = 'Y'
            else:
                format_json_record_C10()
        except Exception as e:
            ws_eof_flag = 'Y'
    ws_eof_flag = 'N'


def format_json_record_C10() -> None:
    """Formats a JSON record."""
    global ws_json_comma, ws_json_line, json_record, daily_date, daily_trans_count, daily_trans_amount, ws_first_record
    daily_date = "20240101"
    daily_trans_count = 10
    daily_trans_amount = decimal.Decimal("100")

    if ws_first_record == 'Y':
        ws_json_comma = ','
    else:
        ws_json_comma = ' '
        ws_first_record = 'Y'
    ws_json_line = f'{ws_json_comma}{{"date":"{daily_date}","transCount":{daily_trans_count},"transAmount":{daily_trans_amount}}}'
    json_record = ws_json_line
    json_export_file.write(json_record + "
")


def account_maintenance_C10() -> None:
    """Performs account maintenance procedures."""
    dormant_account_check_C10()
    escheatment_processing_C10()
    account_closure_C10()
    account_reactivation_C10()


def dormant_account_check_C10() -> None:
    """Checks for dormant accounts."""
    global ws_eof_flag, account_file, ws_account_rec
    ws_eof_flag = 'N'

    while ws_eof_flag != 'Y':
        try:
            ws_account_rec = account_file.readline().strip()
            if not ws_account_rec:
                ws_eof_flag = 'Y'
            else:
                check_activity_C10()
        except Exception as e:
            ws_eof_flag = 'Y'
    ws_eof_flag = 'N'


def check_activity_C10() -> None:
    """Checks account activity."""
    global ws_days_inactive, ws_process_date, acct_last_activity, acct_status
    ws_process_date = "20240101"
    acct_last_activity = "20230101"

    ws_days_inactive = int(ws_process_date) - int(acct_last_activity)
    if ws_days_inactive > 365:
        acct_status = 'D'
        mark_dormant_C10()


def mark_dormant_C10() -> None:
    """Marks an account as dormant."""
    global acct_status_desc, ws_process_date, acct_dormant_date
    acct_status_desc = 'DORMANT'
    ws_process_date = "20240101"
    acct_dormant_date = ws_process_date
    account_record = {}
    send_dormant_notice_C10()


def send_dormant_notice_C10() -> None:
    """Sends a dormant account notice."""
    global ws_notif_type, ws_notif_channel, ws_notif_subject
    ws_notif_type = 'dormant_notice'
    ws_notif_channel = 'MAIL'
    ws_notif_subject = 'Important: Your account is dormant'
    send_notification_C10()


def escheatment_processing_C10() -> None:
    """Processes escheatment of accounts."""
    global ws_eof_flag, account_file, ws_account_rec, acct_status
    ws_eof_flag = 'N'

    while ws_eof_flag != 'Y':
        try:
            ws_account_rec = account_file.readline().strip()
            if not ws_account_rec:
                ws_eof_flag = 'Y'
            else:
                acct_status = 'D'
                if acct_status == 'D':
                    check_escheatment_C10()
        except Exception as e:
            ws_eof_flag = 'Y'
    ws_eof_flag = 'N'


def check_escheatment_C10() -> None:
    """Checks if an account is eligible for escheatment."""
    global ws_dormant_years, ws_process_date, acct_dormant_date, ws_escheat_years
    ws_process_date = "20240101"
    acct_dormant_date = "20230101"
    ws_escheat_years = 2

    ws_dormant_years = (int(ws_process_date) - int(acct_dormant_date)) / 365
    if ws_dormant_years >= ws_escheat_years:
        escheat_account_C10()


def escheat_account_C10() -> None:
    """Escheats an account."""
    global acct_status, acct_balance, ws_escheat_amount, account_record
    acct_status = 'E'
    acct_balance = decimal.Decimal("1000")
    ws_escheat_amount = acct_balance
    acct_balance = decimal.Decimal("0")
    create_escheat_record_C10()


def create_escheat_record_C10() -> None:
    """Creates an escheat record."""
    global ws_escheat_record, acct_id, ws_escheat_amount, ws_process_date, acct_owner_name, acct_owner_address, escheat_account, escheat_amount, escheat_date, escheat_owner, escheat_address, escheat_record
    ws_escheat_record = {}
    acct_id = "12345"
    ws_escheat_amount = decimal.Decimal("1000")
    ws_process_date = "20240101"
    acct_owner_name = "John Doe"
    acct_owner_address = "123 Main St"

    escheat_account = acct_id
    escheat_amount = ws_escheat_amount
    escheat_date = ws_process_date
    escheat_owner = acct_owner_name
    escheat_address = acct_owner_address
    escheat_record = ws_escheat_record


def account_closure_C10() -> None:
    """Processes account closures."""
    global ws_close_request
    ws_close_request = 'Y'

    if ws_close_request == 'Y':
        validate_closure_C10()
        global ws_closure_valid
        if ws_closure_valid == 'Y':
            process_closure_C10()
        else:
            reject_closure_C10()


def validate_closure_C10() -> None:
    """Validates an account closure request."""
    global ws_closure_valid, acct_balance, acct_pending_trans, acct_loan_link, ws_closure_reject
    ws_closure_valid = 'Y'
    acct_balance = decimal.Decimal("100")
    acct_pending_trans = 0
    acct_loan_link = ''

    if acct_balance < 0:
        ws_closure_valid = 'N'
        ws_closure_reject = 'NEGATIVE BALANCE'
    if acct_pending_trans > 0:
        ws_closure_valid = 'N'
        ws_closure_reject = 'PENDING TRANSACTIONS'
    if acct_loan_link != '':
        ws_closure_valid = 'N'
        ws_closure_reject = 'LINKED LOAN EXISTS'


def process_closure_C10() -> None:
    """Processes an account closure."""
    global acct_balance, ws_final_balance, acct_status, ws_process_date, acct_close_date, account_record
    acct_balance = decimal.Decimal("100")
    ws_final_balance = acct_balance
    disburse_balance_C10()
    acct_status = 'C'
    ws_process_date = "20240101"
    acct_close_date = ws_process_date
    account_record = {}
    archive_account_C10()


def disburse_balance_C10() -> None:
    """Disburses the account balance."""
    global ws_final_balance, check_record, acct_id, check_from_account, check_amount, check_memo, acct_owner_name, check_payee, ws_check_record
    ws_final_balance = decimal.Decimal("100")

    if ws_final_balance > 0:
        ws_check_record = {}
        acct_id = "12345"
        check_from_account = acct_id
        check_amount = ws_final_balance
        check_memo = 'ACCOUNT CLOSURE'
        acct_owner_name = "John Doe"
        check_payee = acct_owner_name
        check_record = ws_check_record


def archive_account_C10() -> None:
    """Archives the closed account."""
    global ws_archive_record, ws_account_rec, archive_account_data, ws_process_date, archive_date, archive_retention, archive_record
    ws_archive_record = {}
    ws_account_rec = {}
    archive_account_data = ws_account_rec
    ws_process_date = "20240101"
    archive_date = ws_process_date

    archive_retention = int(ws_process_date) + 2555
    archive_record = ws_archive_record


def reject_closure_C10() -> None:
    """Rejects an account closure request."""
    global ws_notif_type, ws_notif_channel, ws_closure_reject, ws_notif_subject
    ws_notif_type = 'closure_reject'
    ws_notif_channel = 'EMAIL'
    ws_closure_reject = 'Insufficient Funds'
    ws_notif_subject = f'Closure rejected: {ws_closure_reject}'
    send_notification_C10()


def account_reactivation_C10() -> None:
    """Processes account reactivations."""
    global ws_reactivate_request
    ws_reactivate_request = 'Y'

    if ws_reactivate_request == 'Y':
        validate_reactivation_C10()
        global ws_react_valid
        if ws_react_valid == 'Y':
            process_

def card_blocking_C11() -> None:
    """Blocks a card."""
    global CARD_STATUS, WS_BLOCK_REASON, CARD_BLOCK_REASON, WS_PROCESS_DATE, CARD_BLOCK_DATE, WS_CARD_RECORD, WS_NOTIF_TYPE, WS_NOTIF_CHANNEL, WS_NOTIF_BODY
    CARD_STATUS = 'B'
    CARD_BLOCK_REASON  = None  # TODO: was WS_BLOCK_REASON
    CARD_BLOCK_DATE  = None  # TODO: was WS_PROCESS_DATE
    # REWRITE card_record FROM ws_card_record
    WS_NOTIF_TYPE = 'card_blocked'
    WS_NOTIF_CHANNEL = 'SMS'
    WS_NOTIF_BODY = 'Your card has been blocked: ' + WS_BLOCK_REASON
    send_notification_C11()

def wire_transfer_C11() -> None:
    """Processes a wire transfer."""
    global WS_WIRE_VALID, WS_OFAC_CLEAR
    validate_wire_request_C11()
    if WS_WIRE_VALID == 'Y':
        ofac_screening_C11()
        if WS_OFAC_CLEAR == 'Y':
            process_wire_C11()
            send_confirmation_C11()
        else:
            reject_wire_C11()

def validate_wire_request_C11() -> None:
    """Validates a wire transfer request."""
    global WS_WIRE_VALID, WS_WIRE_AMOUNT, WS_ACCOUNT_BALANCE, WS_WIRE_REJECT, WS_BENEFICIARY_ACCOUNT, WS_CTR_REQUIRED
    WS_WIRE_VALID = 'Y'
    if WS_WIRE_AMOUNT <= 0:
        WS_WIRE_VALID = 'N'
        WS_WIRE_REJECT = 'INVALID AMOUNT'
    if WS_WIRE_AMOUNT > WS_ACCOUNT_BALANCE:
        WS_WIRE_VALID = 'N'
        WS_WIRE_REJECT = 'INSUFFICIENT FUNDS'
    if WS_BENEFICIARY_ACCOUNT == SPACES:
        WS_WIRE_VALID = 'N'
        WS_WIRE_REJECT = 'BENEFICIARY REQUIRED'
    if WS_WIRE_AMOUNT > 10000:
        WS_CTR_REQUIRED = 'Y'

def ofac_screening_C11() -> None:
    """Screens the wire transfer against OFAC."""
    global WS_OFAC_CLEAR, WS_BENEFICIARY_NAME, OFAC_SEARCH_NAME, OFAC_REQUEST, OFAC_RESPONSE, OFAC_MATCH_FOUND, OFAC_MATCH_SCORE, WS_WIRE_REJECT, WS_BENEFICIARY_BANK, OFAC_SEARCH_BANK
    WS_OFAC_CLEAR = 'Y'
    OFAC_SEARCH_NAME  = None  # TODO: was WS_BENEFICIARY_NAME
    # CALL 'OFACSRCH' USING ofac_request ofac_response
    if OFAC_MATCH_FOUND == 'Y':
        if OFAC_MATCH_SCORE >= 85:
            WS_OFAC_CLEAR = 'N'
            WS_WIRE_REJECT = 'OFAC MATCH'
    OFAC_SEARCH_BANK  = None  # TODO: was WS_BENEFICIARY_BANK
    # CALL 'OFACSRCH' USING ofac_request ofac_response
    if OFAC_MATCH_FOUND == 'Y':
        if OFAC_MATCH_SCORE >= 85:
            WS_OFAC_CLEAR = 'N'
            WS_WIRE_REJECT = 'BANK OFAC MATCH'

def process_wire_C11() -> None:
    """Processes the wire transfer."""
    debit_originator_C11()
    create_wire_message_C11()
    transmit_wire_C11()
    record_wire_C11()

def debit_originator_C11() -> None:
    """Debits the originator's account."""
    global WS_WIRE_AMOUNT, WS_ACCOUNT_BALANCE, WS_WIRE_FEE
    WS_ACCOUNT_BALANCE -= None  # TODO: was WS_WIRE_AMOUNT
    WS_ACCOUNT_BALANCE -= None  # TODO: was WS_WIRE_FEE
    update_account_C11()

def create_wire_message_C11() -> None:
    """TODO"""
    global WS_SWIFT_MESSAGE, WS_SWIFT_RESPONSE, SWIFT_STATUS, WS_WIRE_STATUS
    # CALL 'SWIFTSEND' USING ws_swift_message ws_swift_response
    if SWIFT_STATUS == 'ACK':
        WS_WIRE_STATUS = 'SENT'
    else:
        WS_WIRE_STATUS = 'FAILED'
        reverse_debit_C11()

def record_wire_C11() -> None:
    """TODO"""
    global WS_WIRE_AMOUNT, WS_ACCOUNT_BALANCE, WS_WIRE_FEE
    WS_ACCOUNT_BALANCE += None  # TODO: was WS_WIRE_AMOUNT
    WS_ACCOUNT_BALANCE += None  # TODO: was WS_WIRE_FEE
    update_account_C11()

def send_confirmation_C11() -> None:
    """Sends a confirmation notification for the wire transfer."""
    global WS_NOTIF_TYPE, WS_NOTIF_CHANNEL, WS_WIRE_REF, WS_NOTIF_SUBJECT
    WS_NOTIF_TYPE = 'wire_confirm'
    WS_NOTIF_CHANNEL = 'EMAIL'
    WS_NOTIF_SUBJECT = 'Wire transfer ' + WS_WIRE_REF + ' completed'
    send_notification_C11()

def reject_wire_C11() -> None:
    """TODO"""
    receive_ach_file_C11()
    validate_ach_entries_C11()
    process_ach_credits_C11()
    process_ach_debits_C11()
    generate_ach_return_C11()

def receive_ach_file_C11() -> None:
        """
    global WS_ACH_ENTRY_VALID, ACH_ROUTING, WS_ACH_RETURN_CODE, ACH_ACCOUNT, ACH_AMOUNT, WS_VALID_ENTRIES, WS_INVALID_ENTRIES
    WS_ACH_ENTRY_VALID = 'Y'
    if not ACH_ROUTING.isnumeric():
        WS_ACH_ENTRY_VALID = 'N'
        WS_ACH_RETURN_CODE = 'R03'
    if ACH_ACCOUNT == SPACES:
        WS_ACH_ENTRY_VALID = 'N'
        WS_ACH_RETURN_CODE = 'R04'
    if ACH_AMOUNT <= 0:
        WS_ACH_ENTRY_VALID = 'N'
        WS_ACH_RETURN_CODE = 'R06'
    if WS_ACH_ENTRY_VALID == 'Y':
        WS_VALID_ENTRIES += 1
    else:
        WS_INVALID_ENTRIES += 1

def process_ach_credits_C11() -> None:
    """TODO"""
    global ACH_ACCOUNT, WS_SEARCH_KEY, WS_FOUND_FLAG, ACH_AMOUNT, WS_ACCOUNT_BALANCE, WS_CREDITS_POSTED, WS_TOTAL_CREDITS, WS_ACH_RETURN_CODE
    WS_SEARCH_KEY  = None  # TODO: was ACH_ACCOUNT
    search_account_C11()
    if WS_FOUND_FLAG == 'Y':
        WS_ACCOUNT_BALANCE += None  # TODO: was ACH_AMOUNT
        update_account_C11()
        WS_CREDITS_POSTED += 1
        WS_TOTAL_CREDITS += None  # TODO: was ACH_AMOUNT
    else:
        WS_ACH_RETURN_CODE = 'R04'
        create_return_entry_C11()

def process_ach_debits_C11() -> None:
    """TODO"""
    global ACH_ACCOUNT, WS_SEARCH_KEY, WS_FOUND_FLAG, WS_ACCOUNT_BALANCE, ACH_AMOUNT, WS_DEBITS_POSTED, WS_TOTAL_DEBITS, WS_ACH_RETURN_CODE
    WS_SEARCH_KEY  = None  # TODO: was ACH_ACCOUNT
    search_account_C11()
    if WS_FOUND_FLAG == 'Y':
        if WS_ACCOUNT_BALANCE >= ACH_AMOUNT:
            WS_ACCOUNT_BALANCE -= None  # TODO: was ACH_AMOUNT
            update_account_C11()
            WS_DEBITS_POSTED += 1
            WS_TOTAL_DEBITS += None  # TODO: was ACH_AMOUNT
        else:
            WS_ACH_RETURN_CODE = 'R01'
            create_return_entry_C11()
    else:
        WS_ACH_RETURN_CODE = 'R04'
        create_return_entry_C11()

def generate_ach_return_C11() -> None:
    """Generates the ACH return file if there are returns."""
    global WS_RETURN_COUNT
    if WS_RETURN_COUNT > 0:
        create_return_file_C11()

def create_return_entry_C11() -> None:
        """
    # OPEN OUTPUT ach_return_file
    write_return_header_C11()
    write_return_entries_C11()
    write_return_trailer_C11()
    # CLOSE ach_return_file
    pass

def write_return_header_C11() -> None:
    """TODO"""
    global WS_RETURN_IDX, WS_RETURN_COUNT
    WS_RETURN_IDX = 0
    while WS_RETURN_IDX > WS_RETURN_COUNT:
        # WRITE ach_return_record FROM ws_return_entry(ws_return_idx)
        WS_RETURN_IDX += 1

def write_return_trailer_C11() -> None:
    """Writes the trailer record to the ACH return file."""
    global WS_RETURN_TRAILER, RETURN_RECORD_TYPE, WS_RETURN_COUNT, RETURN_ENTRY_COUNT, WS_RETURN_TOTAL, RETURN_TOTAL_AMOUNT
    # INITIALIZE ws_return_trailer
    RETURN_RECORD_TYPE = '9'
    RETURN_ENTRY_COUNT  = None  # TODO: was WS_RETURN_COUNT
    RETURN_TOTAL_AMOUNT  = None  # TODO: was WS_RETURN_TOTAL
    # WRITE ach_return_record FROM ws_return_trailer
    pass

def statement_generation_C11() -> None:
    """Generates account statements."""
    prepare_statement_data_C11()
    generate_account_summary_C11()
    generate_transaction_detail_C11()
    calculate_statement_totals_C11()
    format_statement_C11()
    deliver_statement_C11()

def validate_stop_request_C12(ws_check_number: int, ws_check_already_cleared: str, ws_stop_valid: str, ws_stop_reject: str) -> Tuple[str, str]:
    """Validates stop request."""
    if ws_check_number == 0:
        ws_stop_valid = 'N'
        ws_stop_reject = 'CHECK NUMBER REQUIRED'
    if ws_check_already_cleared == 'Y':
        ws_stop_valid = 'N'
        ws_stop_reject = 'CHECK ALREADY CLEARED'
    return ws_stop_valid, ws_stop_reject

def create_stop_order_C12(acct_id: str, ws_check_number: str, ws_check_amount: Decimal, ws_payee_name: str, ws_process_date: str, ws_stop_record: WsStopRecordC12) -> WsStopRecordC12:
    """Creates a stop order."""
    ws_stop_record = WsStopRecordC12()
    ws_stop_record.stop_account = acct_id
    ws_stop_record.stop_check_number = ws_check_number
    ws_stop_record.stop_amount = ws_check_amount
    ws_stop_record.stop_payee = ws_payee_name
    ws_stop_record.stop_effective_date = ws_process_date
    ws_stop_record.stop_expiry_date = str(int(ws_process_date) + 180)
    ws_stop_record.stop_status = 'A'
    return ws_stop_record

def apply_stop_fee_C12(ws_stop_payment_fee: Decimal, ws_account_balance: Decimal, ws_notif_type: str, ws_notif_channel: str, ws_check_number: str, ws_notif_subject: str) -> Tuple[Decimal, str, str, str]:
    """Applies stop fee to the account."""
    ws_account_balance -= ws_stop_payment_fee
    update_account_C12(ws_account_balance)
    ws_notif_type = 'stop_payment'
    ws_notif_channel = 'EMAIL'
    ws_notif_subject = f'Stop payment placed on check #{ws_check_number}'
    send_notification_C12(ws_notif_type, ws_notif_channel, ws_notif_subject)
    return ws_account_balance, ws_notif_type, ws_notif_channel, ws_notif_subject

def safe_deposit_box_C12(ws_rental_request: str, ws_access_request: str, ws_drilling_request: str) -> None:
    """Manages safe deposit box procedures."""
    box_rental_C12(ws_rental_request)
    box_access_C12(ws_access_request)
    box_drilling_C12(ws_drilling_request)
    box_billing_C12()

def box_rental_C12(ws_rental_request: str) -> None:
    """Handles box rental requests."""
    if ws_rental_request == 'Y':
        ws_box_available = check_availability_C12()
        if ws_box_available == 'Y':
            assign_box_C12()
            create_rental_agreement_C12()

def check_availability_C12() -> str:
    """Checks the availability of a safe deposit box."""
    ws_box_available = 'N'
    ws_assigned_box = ""
    for ws_box_idx in range(1, ws_total_boxes + 1):
        if box_status[ws_box_idx] == 'A':
            if box_size[ws_box_idx] == ws_requested_size:
                ws_box_available = 'Y'
                ws_assigned_box = str(ws_box_idx)
                break
    return ws_box_available

def assign_box_C12() -> None:
    """Assigns a safe deposit box to a customer."""
    box_status[int(ws_assigned_box)] = 'R'
    box_renter[int(ws_assigned_box)] = ws_customer_id
    box_rental_date[int(ws_assigned_box)] = ws_process_date

def create_rental_agreement_C12() -> WsRentalAgreementC12:
    """Creates a rental agreement for the safe deposit box."""
    ws_rental_agreement = WsRentalAgreementC12()
    ws_rental_agreement.rental_box_number = ws_assigned_box
    ws_rental_agreement.rental_customer = ws_customer_id
    ws_rental_agreement.rental_start_date = ws_process_date
    ws_rental_agreement.rental_annual_fee = ws_box_size_fee[ws_requested_size]
    return ws_rental_agreement

def box_access_C12(ws_access_request: str) -> None:
    """Handles box access requests."""
    if ws_access_request == 'Y':
        ws_renter_verified = verify_renter_C12()
        if ws_renter_verified == 'Y':
            log_access_C12()
            escort_to_vault_C12()

def verify_renter_C12() -> str:
    """Verifies the renter's identity for box access."""
    ws_renter_verified = 'N'
    if box_renter[int(ws_box_number)] == ws_customer_id:
        if ws_id_verified == 'Y':
            if ws_key_verified == 'Y':
                ws_renter_verified = 'Y'
    return ws_renter_verified

def log_access_C12() -> WsAccessLogC12:
    """Logs the box access."""
    ws_access_log = WsAccessLogC12()
    ws_access_log.access_box_number = ws_box_number
    ws_access_log.access_customer = ws_customer_id
    ws_access_log.access_date = ws_process_date
    ws_access_log.access_time = "CURRENT_TIME"  # Replace with actual current time function
    ws_access_log.access_type = 'ENTRY'
    return ws_access_log

def escort_to_vault_C12() -> None:
    """Grants vault access to the renter."""
    ws_display_msg = 'VAULT ACCESS GRANTED'
    print(ws_display_msg)

def box_drilling_C12(ws_drilling_request: str) -> None:
    """Handles box drilling requests."""
    if ws_drilling_request == 'Y':
        ws_drilling_authorized = validate_drilling_auth_C12()
        if ws_drilling_authorized == 'Y':
            schedule_drilling_C12()
            notify_renter_C12()

def validate_drilling_auth_C12() -> str:
    """Validates the authorization for drilling a safe deposit box."""
    ws_drilling_authorized = 'N'
    if ws_rent_delinquent_months >= 12:
        ws_drilling_authorized = 'Y'
    if ws_court_order == 'Y':
        ws_drilling_authorized = 'Y'
    if ws_deceased_renter == 'Y':
        if ws_executor_verified == 'Y':
            ws_drilling_authorized = 'Y'
    return ws_drilling_authorized

def schedule_drilling_C12() -> WsDrillingRecordC12:
    """Schedules the drilling of a safe deposit box."""
    ws_drilling_record = WsDrillingRecordC12()
    ws_drilling_record.drill_box_number = ws_box_number
    ws_drilling_record.drill_reason = ws_drilling_reason
    ws_drilling_record.drill_scheduled_date = str(int(ws_process_date) + 30)
    return ws_drilling_record

def notify_renter_C12() -> None:
    """Notifies the renter about the box drilling."""
    ws_notif_type = 'box_drilling'
    ws_notif_channel = 'MAIL'
    ws_notif_subject = 'Important notice regarding your safe deposit box'
    send_notification_C12(ws_notif_type, ws_notif_channel, ws_notif_subject)

def box_billing_C12() -> None:
    """Handles billing for safe deposit boxes."""
    for ws_box_idx in range(1, ws_total_boxes + 1):
        if box_status[ws_box_idx] == 'R':
            if box_renewal_due[ws_box_idx] == 'Y':
                charge_annual_fee_C12(ws_box_idx)

def charge_annual_fee_C12(ws_box_idx: int) -> None:
    """Charges the annual fee for a safe deposit box."""
    ws_customer_id = box_renter[ws_box_idx]
    ws_fee_amount = box_annual_fee[ws_box_idx]
    ws_account_balance -= ws_fee_amount
    update_account_C12(ws_account_balance)
    box_next_renewal[ws_box_idx] = box_next_renewal[ws_box_idx] + 10000

def merchant_services_C12(ws_auth_request: WsAuthRequestC12, ws_capture_request: str, ws_chargeback_request: str) -> None:
    """Manages merchant services procedures."""
    process_authorization_C12(ws_auth_request)
    capture_transaction_C12(ws_capture_request)
    process_settlement_C12()
    handle_chargeback_C12(ws_chargeback_request)

def process_authorization_C12(ws_auth_request: WsAuthRequestC12) -> None:
    """Processes authorization requests."""
    ws_card_valid = validate_card_C12(ws_auth_request)
    if ws_card_valid == 'Y':
        ws_fraud_approved = check_fraud_score_C12(ws_auth_request)
        if ws_fraud_approved == 'Y':
            ws_credit_available = check_available_credit_C12(ws_auth_request.auth_card_number, ws_auth_request.auth_amount)
            if ws_credit_available == 'Y':
                approve_auth_C12(ws_auth_request.auth_amount, ws_auth_request.auth_card_number)
            else:
                decline_auth_C12()
        else:
            decline_auth_C12()
    else:
        decline_auth_C12()

def validate_card_C12(ws_auth_request: WsAuthRequestC12) -> str:
    """Validates the card details."""
    ws_card_valid = 'N'
    ws_luhn_valid = check_luhn_C12(ws_auth_request.auth_card_number)
    if ws_luhn_valid == 'Y':
        ws_not_expired = check_expiry_C12(ws_auth_request.auth_expiry_date)
        if ws_not_expired == 'Y':
            ws_cvv_valid = check_cvv_C12(ws_auth_request.auth_card_number, ws_auth_request.auth_cvv)
            if ws_cvv_valid == 'Y':
                ws_card_valid = 'Y'
    return ws_card_valid

def check_luhn_C12(ws_auth_card_number: str) -> str:
    """Checks the card number using the Luhn algorithm."""
    ws_luhn_sum = 0
    for ws_luhn_idx in range(16, 0, -1):
        ws_luhn_digit = int(ws_auth_card_number[ws_luhn_idx - 1])
        if (17 - ws_luhn_idx) % 2 == 0:
            ws_luhn_digit *= 2
            if ws_luhn_digit > 9:
                ws_luhn_digit -= 9
        ws_luhn_sum += ws_luhn_digit
    if ws_luhn_sum % 10 == 0:
        ws_luhn_valid = 'Y'
    else:
        ws_luhn_valid = 'N'
    return ws_luhn_valid

def check_expiry_C12(ws_auth_expiry_date: str) -> str:
    """Checks if the card has expired."""
    if ws_auth_expiry_date >= ws_process_date:
        ws_not_expired = 'Y'
    else:
        ws_not_expired = 'N'
    return ws_not_expired

def check_cvv_C12(ws_auth_card_number: str, ws_auth_cvv: str) -> str:
    """Checks the CVV."""
    cvv_result = cvvverify_C12(ws_auth_card_number, ws_auth_cvv)
    if cvv_result == 'M':
        ws_cvv_valid = 'Y'
    else:
        ws_cvv_valid = 'N'
    return ws_cvv_valid

def check_fraud_score_C12(ws_auth_request: WsAuthRequestC12) -> str:
    """Checks the fraud score."""
    fraud_response = fraudcheck_C12(ws_auth_request)
    if fraud_response.fraud_score < 70:
        ws_fraud_approved = 'Y'
    else:
        ws_fraud_approved = 'N'
        ws_auth_decline_code = fraud_response.fraud_decline_code
    return ws_fraud_approved

def check_available_credit_C12(ws_auth_card_number: str, ws_auth_amount: Decimal) -> str:
    """Checks the available credit."""
    ws_search_key = ws_auth_card_number
    ws_card_account_rec = read_card_account_file_C12(ws_search_key)
    if ws_card_account_rec.available_credit >= ws_auth_amount:
        ws_credit_available = 'Y'
    else:
        ws_credit_available = 'N'
        ws_auth_decline_code = '51'
    return ws_credit_available

def approve_auth_C12(ws_auth_amount: Decimal, ws_auth_card_number: str) -> None:
    """Approves the authorization."""
    ws_auth_response_code = '00'
    ws_auth_code = generate_auth_code_C12()
    ws_available_credit -= ws_auth_amount
    record_authorization_C12(ws_auth_card_number, ws_auth_amount, ws_auth_code)

def generate_auth_code_C12() -> int:
    """Generates an authorization code."""
    ws_auth_code = int(random_C12() * 999999)
    ws_auth_response_auth_code = str(ws_auth_code)
    return ws_auth_code

def record_authorization_C12(ws_auth_card_number: str, ws_auth_amount: Decimal, ws_auth_code: int) -> WsAuthRecordC12:
    """Records the authorization details."""
    ws_auth_record = WsAuthRecordC12()
    ws_auth_record.auth_rec_card = ws_auth_card_number
    ws_auth_record.auth_rec_amount = ws_auth_amount
    ws_auth_record.auth_rec_code = str(ws_auth_code)
    ws_auth_record.auth_rec_date = ws_process_date
    ws_auth_record.auth_rec_time = "CURRENT_TIME"  # Replace with actual current time function
    ws_auth_record.auth_rec_merchant = ws_merchant_id
    ws_auth_record.auth_rec_status = 'P'
    return ws_auth_record

def decline_auth_C12() -> WsDeclineRecordC12:
    """Declines the authorization."""
    ws_auth_response_code = ws_auth_decline_code
    ws_decline_record = WsDeclineRecordC12()
    ws_decline_record.decline_rec_card = ws_auth_card_number
    ws_decline_record.decline_rec_amount = ws_auth_amount
    ws_decline_record.decline_rec_code = ws_auth_decline_code
    ws_decline_record.decline_rec_date = ws_process_date
    return ws_decline_record

def capture_transaction_C12(ws_capture_request: str) -> None:
    """Captures a transaction."""
    if ws_capture_request == 'Y':
        ws_auth_valid = validate_auth_code_C12()
        if ws_auth_valid == 'Y':
            create_capture_record_C12()

def validate_auth_code_C12() -> str:
    """Validates the authorization code."""
    ws_auth_valid = 'N'
    ws_auth_rec = read_auth_file_C12(ws_capture_auth_code)
    if ws_auth_rec:
        if ws_auth_rec.auth_rec_status == 'P':
            ws_auth_valid = 'Y'
    else:
        ws_auth_valid = 'N'
    return ws_auth_valid

def create_capture_record_C12() -> WsCaptureRecordC12:
    """Creates a capture record."""
    ws_auth_rec.auth_rec_status = 'C'
    rewrite_auth_record_C12(ws_auth_rec)
    ws_capture_record = WsCaptureRecordC12()
    ws_capture_record.capture_card = ws_auth_rec.auth_rec_card
    ws_capture_record.capture_amount = ws_capture_amount
    ws_capture_record.capture_auth_code = ws_capture_auth_code
    ws_capture_record.capture_date = ws_process_date
    return ws_capture_record

def process_settlement_C12() -> None:
    """Processes settlement."""
    batch_transactions_C12()
    calculate_fees_C12()
    create_funding_record_C12()
    send_settlement_file_C12()

def batch_transactions_C12() -> None:
    """Batches transactions for settlement."""
    ws_batch_total = Decimal("0.00")
    ws_batch_count = 0
    ws_eof_flag = 'N'
    while ws_eof_flag == 'N':
        capture_rec = read_capture_file_C12()
        if capture_rec is None:
            ws_eof_flag = 'Y'
        else:
            if capture_rec.capture_settled == 'N':
                ws_batch_total += capture_rec.capture_amount
                ws_batch_count += 1
                capture_rec.capture_settled = 'Y'
                rewrite_capture_record_C12(capture_rec)
    ws_eof_flag = 'N'

def calculate_fees_C12() -> None:
    """Calculates the fees for settlement."""
    ws_interchange_fee = ws_batch_total * Decimal("0.0175")
    ws_assessment_fee = ws_batch_total * Decimal("0.0015")
    ws_processor_fee = ws_batch_count * Decimal("0.10")
    ws_total_fees = ws_interchange_fee + ws_assessment_fee + ws_processor_fee

def create_funding_record_C12() -> WsFundingRecordC12:
    """Creates a funding record."""
    ws_net_funding = ws_batch_total - ws_total_fees
    ws_funding_record = WsFundingRecordC12()
    ws_funding_record.funding_merchant = ws_merchant_id
    ws_funding_record.funding_amount = ws_net_funding
    ws_funding_record.funding_fees = ws_total_fees
    ws_funding_record.funding_date = str(int(ws_process_date) + 2)
    return ws_funding_record

def send_settlement_file_C12() -> None:
    """Sends the settlement file."""
    open_output_settlement_file_C12()
    write_settlement_header_C12()
    write_settlement_detail_C12()
    write_settlement_trailer_C12()
    close_settlement_file_C12()

def write_settlement_header_C12() -> WsSettleHeaderC12:
    """Writes the settlement header."""
    ws_settle_header = WsSettleHeaderC12()
    ws_settle_header.settle_record_type = 'H'
    ws_settle_header.settle_merchant_id = ws_merchant_id
    ws_settle_header.settle_date = ws_process_date
    return ws_settle_header

def write_settlement_detail_C12() -> None:
    """Writes the settlement detail records."""
    ws_eof_flag = 'N'
    while ws_eof_flag == 'N':
        capture_rec = read_capture_file_C12()
        if capture_rec is None:
            ws_eof_flag = 'Y'
        else:
            if capture_rec.capture_settled == 'Y':
                ws_settle_detail = WsSettleDetailC12()
                ws_settle_detail.settle_record_type = 'D'
                ws_settle_detail.settle_card = capture_rec.capture_card
                ws_settle_detail.settle_amount = capture_rec.capture_amount
                ws_settle_detail.settle_auth_code = capture_rec.capture_auth_code
    ws_eof_flag = 'N'

def write_settlement_trailer_C12() -> WsSettleTrailerC12:
    """Writes the settlement trailer."""
    ws_settle_trailer = WsSettleTrailerC12()
    ws_settle_trailer.settle_record_type = 'T'
    ws_settle_trailer.settle_total_count = ws_batch_count
    ws_settle_trailer.settle_total_amount = ws_batch_total
    return ws_settle_trailer

def handle_chargeback_C12(ws_chargeback_request: str) -> None:
    """Handles chargebacks."""
    if ws_chargeback_request == 'Y':
        receive_chargeback_C12()
        research_transaction_C12()
        respond_to_chargeback_C12()

def receive_chargeback_C12() -> WsChargebackRecordC12:
    """Receives a chargeback."""
    ws_chargeback_record = WsChargebackRecordC12()
    ws_chargeback_record.cb_card = ws_cb_card_number
    ws_chargeback_record.cb_amount = ws_cb_amount
    ws_chargeback_record.cb_reason = ws_cb_reason_code
    ws_chargeback_record.cb_case_id = ws_cb_case_number
    ws_chargeback_record.cb_received_date = ws_process_date
    ws_chargeback_record.cb_status = 'RECEIVED'
    return ws_chargeback_record

def research_transaction_C12() -> None:
    """Researches the original transaction."""
    auth_file = read_auth_file_C12(ws_cb_auth_code)
    if auth_file:
        ws_trans_found = 'Y'
        ws_original_auth = auth_file
    else:
        ws_trans_found = 'N'
        ws_original_auth =None  # TODO: Add value

def logging_utilities_C13() -> None:
    """Calls the logging functions."""
    log_info_C13()
    log_warning_C13()
    log_error_C13()


def log_info_C13() -> None:
    """Logs an info message."""
    global log_level, ws_log_message, log_timestamp, ws_log_entry
    log_level = 'INFO'
    log_timestamp = datetime.now()
    pass


def log_warning_C13() -> None:
    """Logs a warning message."""
    global log_level, ws_log_message, log_timestamp, ws_log_entry
    log_level = 'WARN'
    log_timestamp = datetime.now()
    pass


def log_error_C13() -> None:
    """Logs an error message."""
    global log_level, ws_log_message, log_timestamp, ws_log_entry
    log_level = 'ERROR'
    log_timestamp = datetime.now()
    pass


def error_handling_C13() -> None:
    """Handles errors."""
    format_error_C13()
    display_error_C13()
    write_error_log_C13()


def format_error_C13() -> None:
    """Formats an error message."""
    global ws_formatted_error, ws_error_code, ws_error_msg
    ws_formatted_error = f"ERROR: {ws_error_code} - {ws_error_msg}"


def display_error_C13() -> None:
    """Displays an error message."""
    global ws_formatted_error
    print(ws_formatted_error)


def write_error_log_C13() -> None:
    """Writes an error log."""
    global ws_error_log_rec, err_log_code, err_log_msg, err_log_timestamp, ws_program_name, ws_paragraph_name
    err_log_code = ws_error_code
    err_log_msg = ws_error_msg
    err_log_timestamp = datetime.now()
    pass

def treasury_management_C13() -> None:
    """Manages treasury functions."""
    calculate_cash_position_C13()
    project_cash_flows_C13()
    manage_reserves_C13()
    manage_investments_C13()
    manage_borrowings_C13()


def calculate_cash_position_C13() -> None:
    """Calculates the cash position."""
    global ws_treasury_management
    ws_treasury_management = WSTreasuryManagementC13()
    sum_vault_cash_C13()
    sum_fed_account_C13()
    sum_correspondent_balances_C13()


def sum_vault_cash_C13() -> None:
    """Sums the vault cash."""
    global ws_eof_flag, ws_vault_rec, ws_treasury_management, vault_balance
    ws_eof_flag = "N"
    while ws_eof_flag != 'Y':
        # Simulate reading from vault cash file
        # In real implementation replace with file reading logic
        ws_vault_rec = ""  # Read vault cash record
        if not ws_vault_rec:  # AT END
            ws_eof_flag = 'Y'
        else:  # NOT AT END
            ws_treasury_management.ws_cash_position += vault_balance
    ws_eof_flag = 'N'


def sum_fed_account_C13() -> None:
    """Sums the fed account."""
    global ws_treasury_management, ws_fed_balance
    # Simulate reading from Fed account file
    # In real implementation replace with file reading logic
    ws_fed_balance = Decimal("1000000.00")  # Example balance
    ws_treasury_management.ws_cash_position += ws_fed_balance


def sum_correspondent_balances_C13() -> None:
    """Sums the correspondent balances."""
    global ws_eof_flag, ws_corr_rec, ws_treasury_management, corr_balance
    ws_eof_flag = "N"
    while ws_eof_flag != 'Y':
        # Simulate reading from correspondent file
        # In real implementation replace with file reading logic
        ws_corr_rec = ""  # Read correspondent record
        if not ws_corr_rec:  # AT END
            ws_eof_flag = 'Y'
        else:  # NOT AT END
            ws_treasury_management.ws_cash_position += corr_balance
    ws_eof_flag = 'N'


def project_cash_flows_C13() -> None:
    """Projects cash flows."""
    global ws_treasury_management
    ws_treasury_management.ws_projected_inflows = Decimal("0.00")
    ws_treasury_management.ws_projected_outflows = Decimal("0.00")
    project_loan_payments_C13()
    project_deposit_flows_C13()
    project_investment_maturities_C13()
    ws_treasury_management.ws_net_position = (
        ws_treasury_management.ws_cash_position + 0  # TODO
        ws_treasury_management.ws_projected_inflows - 0  # TODO
        ws_treasury_management.ws_projected_outflows
    )


def project_loan_payments_C13() -> None:
    """Projects loan payments."""
    global ws_eof_flag, ws_loan_pmt_rec, ws_treasury_management, loan_pmt_date, loan_pmt_amount, ws_projection_date
    ws_eof_flag = "N"
    while ws_eof_flag != 'Y':
        # Simulate reading from loan schedule file
        # In real implementation replace with file reading logic
        ws_loan_pmt_rec = ""  # Read loan payment record
        if not ws_loan_pmt_rec:  # AT END
            ws_eof_flag = 'Y'
        else:  # NOT AT END
            if loan_pmt_date <= ws_projection_date:
                ws_treasury_management.ws_projected_inflows += loan_pmt_amount
    ws_eof_flag = 'N'


def project_deposit_flows_C13() -> None:
    """Projects deposit flows."""
    global ws_treasury_management, ws_avg_daily_deposits, ws_avg_daily_withdrawals, ws_projection_days, ws_expected_deposits, ws_expected_withdrawals
    ws_expected_deposits = ws_avg_daily_deposits * ws_projection_days
    ws_expected_withdrawals = ws_avg_daily_withdrawals * ws_projection_days
    ws_treasury_management.ws_projected_inflows += ws_expected_deposits
    ws_treasury_management.ws_projected_outflows += ws_expected_withdrawals


def project_investment_maturities_C13() -> None:
    """Projects investment maturities."""
    global ws_eof_flag, ws_inv_rec, ws_treasury_management, inv_maturity_date, inv_par_value, ws_projection_date
    ws_eof_flag = "N"
    while ws_eof_flag != 'Y':
        # Simulate reading from investment file
        # In real implementation replace with file reading logic
        ws_inv_rec = ""  # Read investment record
        if not ws_inv_rec:  # AT END
            ws_eof_flag = 'Y'
        else:  # NOT AT END
            if inv_maturity_date <= ws_projection_date:
                ws_treasury_management.ws_projected_inflows += inv_par_value
    ws_eof_flag = 'N'


def manage_reserves_C13() -> None:
    """Manages reserves."""
    global ws_reserve_deficiency
    calculate_reserve_requirement_C13()
    check_reserve_position_C13()
    if ws_reserve_deficiency == 'Y':
        cover_reserve_shortfall_C13()
    else:
        invest_excess_reserves_C13()


def calculate_reserve_requirement_C13() -> None:
    """Calculates the reserve requirement."""
    global ws_treasury_management, ws_reserve_requirement, ws_total_deposits, ws_reserve_ratio
    ws_treasury_management.ws_reserve_requirement = ws_total_deposits * ws_reserve_ratio


def check_reserve_position_C13() -> None:
    """Checks the reserve position."""
    global ws_treasury_management, ws_excess_reserves, ws_fed_balance, ws_reserve_requirement, ws_reserve_deficiency
    ws_treasury_management.ws_excess_reserves = ws_fed_balance - ws_treasury_management.ws_reserve_requirement
    if ws_treasury_management.ws_excess_reserves < 0:
        ws_reserve_deficiency = 'Y'
    else:
        ws_reserve_deficiency = 'N'


def cover_reserve_shortfall_C13() -> None:
    """Covers the reserve shortfall."""
    global ws_treasury_management, ws_excess_reserves, ws_shortfall_amount
    ws_shortfall_amount = 0 - ws_treasury_management.ws_excess_reserves
    borrow_fed_funds_C13()


def borrow_fed_funds_C13() -> None:
    """Borrows fed funds."""
    global ws_fed_funds_transaction, ff_trans_type, ws_shortfall_amount, ff_amount, ws_fed_funds_rate, ff_rate, ws_process_date, ff_settle_date, ff_maturity_date
    ff_trans_type = 'BORROW'
    ff_amount = ws_shortfall_amount
    ff_rate = ws_fed_funds_rate
    ff_settle_date = ws_process_date
    ff_maturity_date = ws_process_date + 1
    pass

def invest_excess_reserves_C13() -> None:
    """Invests excess reserves."""
    global ws_treasury_management, ws_excess_reserves, ws_min_invest_amount
    if ws_treasury_management.ws_excess_reserves > ws_min_invest_amount:
        sell_fed_funds_C13()


def sell_fed_funds_C13() -> None:
    """Sells fed funds."""
    global ws_fed_funds_transaction, ff_trans_type, ws_treasury_management, ws_excess_reserves, ff_amount, ws_fed_funds_rate, ff_rate, ws_process_date, ff_settle_date, ff_maturity_date
    ff_trans_type = 'SELL'
    ff_amount = ws_treasury_management.ws_excess_reserves
    ff_rate = ws_fed_funds_rate
    ff_settle_date = ws_process_date
    ff_maturity_date = ws_process_date + 1
    pass

def manage_investments_C13() -> None:
    """Manages investments."""
    review_investment_portfolio_C13()
    execute_investment_strategy_C13()
    mark_to_market_C13()


def review_investment_portfolio_C13() -> None:
    """Reviews the investment portfolio."""
    global ws_eof_flag, ws_inv_rec, ws_investment_pool, ws_avg_yield, ws_avg_duration, ws_total_yield, ws_total_duration, ws_inv_count, inv_market_value, inv_yield, inv_duration, ws_treasury_management
    ws_treasury_management.ws_investment_pool = Decimal("0.00")
    ws_avg_yield = Decimal("0.00")
    ws_avg_duration = Decimal("0.00")
    ws_total_yield = Decimal("0.00")
    ws_total_duration = Decimal("0.00")
    ws_inv_count = 0
    ws_eof_flag = "N"
    while ws_eof_flag != 'Y':
        # Simulate reading from investment file
        # In real implementation replace with file reading logic
        ws_inv_rec = ""  # Read investment record
        if not ws_inv_rec:  # AT END
            ws_eof_flag = 'Y'
        else:  # NOT AT END
            ws_treasury_management.ws_investment_pool += inv_market_value
            ws_total_yield += inv_yield
            ws_total_duration += inv_duration
            ws_inv_count += 1
    if ws_inv_count > 0:
        ws_avg_yield = ws_total_yield / ws_inv_count
        ws_avg_duration = ws_total_duration / ws_inv_count
    ws_eof_flag = 'N'


def execute_investment_strategy_C13() -> None:
    """Executes the investment strategy."""
    global ws_rate_outlook
    if ws_rate_outlook == 'RISING':
        shorten_duration_C13()
    elif ws_rate_outlook == 'FALLING':
        extend_duration_C13()
    elif ws_rate_outlook == 'STABLE':
        maintain_position_C13()

def shorten_duration_C13() -> None:
    """Shortens portfolio duration."""
    print('STRATEGY: SHORTENING PORTFOLIO DURATION')


def extend_duration_C13() -> None:
    """Extends portfolio duration."""
    print('STRATEGY: EXTENDING PORTFOLIO DURATION')


def maintain_position_C13() -> None:
    """Maintains current position."""
    print('STRATEGY: MAINTAINING CURRENT POSITION')

def mark_to_market_C13() -> None:
    """Marks investments to market."""
    global ws_eof_flag, ws_inv_rec, inv_market_value, inv_par_value, ws_market_price, inv_unrealized_gl, inv_book_value, inv_cusip
    ws_eof_flag = "N"
    while ws_eof_flag != 'Y':
        # Simulate reading from investment file
        # In real implementation replace with file reading logic
        ws_inv_rec = ""  # Read investment record
        if not ws_inv_rec:  # AT END
            ws_eof_flag = 'Y'
        else:  # NOT AT END
            get_market_price_C13()
            inv_market_value = inv_par_value * ws_market_price / 100
            inv_unrealized_gl = inv_market_value - inv_book_value
            pass
    ws_eof_flag = 'N'


def get_market_price_C13() -> None:
    """Gets the market price."""
    global inv_cusip, ws_cusip_lookup, ws_market_price
    ws_cusip_lookup = inv_cusip
    ws_market_price = Decimal("100.00")  # Simulate bond price call
    pass

def manage_borrowings_C13() -> None:
    """Manages borrowings."""
    review_borrowing_capacity_C13()
    optimize_funding_mix_C13()
    manage_maturities_C13()

def review_borrowing_capacity_C13() -> None:
    """Reviews borrowing capacity."""
    global ws_treasury_management, ws_fhlb_capacity, ws_repo_capacity, ws_credit_line_avail
    ws_treasury_management.ws_borrowing_capacity = Decimal("0.00")
    ws_treasury_management.ws_borrowing_capacity += ws_fhlb_capacity
    ws_treasury_management.ws_borrowing_capacity += ws_repo_capacity
    ws_treasury_management.ws_borrowing_capacity += ws_credit_line_avail

def optimize_funding_mix_C13() -> None:
    """Optimizes funding mix."""
    global ws_deposit_cost, ws_total_int_expense, ws_total_deposits, ws_wholesale_rate
    if ws_total_deposits > 0:
      ws_deposit_cost = ws_total_int_expense / ws_total_deposits * 100
    if ws_deposit_cost > ws_wholesale_rate:
        print('CONSIDER WHOLESALE FUNDING')

def manage_maturities_C13() -> None:
    """Manages maturities."""
    global ws_eof_flag, ws_borrow_rec, borrow_maturity, ws_process_date
    ws_eof_flag = "N"
    while ws_eof_flag != 'Y':
        # Simulate reading from borrowing file
        # In real implementation replace with file reading logic
        ws_borrow_rec = ""  # Read borrowing record
        if not ws_borrow_rec:  # AT END
            ws_eof_flag = 'Y'
        else:  # NOT AT END
            if borrow_maturity <= ws_process_date + 7:
                rollover_decision_C13()
    ws_eof_flag = 'N'

def rollover_decision_C13() -> None:
    """Decides whether to rollover a borrowing."""
    global ws_treasury_management, borrow_amount
    if ws_treasury_management.ws_cash_position

def perform_37300_intercompany_recon_C15() -> None:
    """Performs intercompany reconciliation."""
    perform_37310_load_ic_balances_C15()
    perform_37320_match_ic_pairs_C15()
    perform_37330_report_ic_differences_C15()

def perform_37310_load_ic_balances_C15() -> None:
    """Loads intercompany balances."""
    global WS_IC_COUNTC15, WS_EOF_FLAGC15, INTERCOMPANY_FILEC15, WS_IC_BALANCEC15, WS_IC_ARRAYC15
    WS_IC_COUNTC15 = 0
    WS_EOF_FLAGC15 = 'N'
    while WS_EOF_FLAGC15 != 'Y':
        try:
            WS_IC_BALANCEC15 = INTERCOMPANY_FILEC15
            WS_IC_COUNTC15 += 1
            WS_IC_ARRAYC15.append(WS_IC_BALANCEC15)
        except:
            WS_EOF_FLAGC15 = 'Y'
    WS_EOF_FLAGC15 = 'N'

def perform_37320_match_ic_pairs_C15() -> None:
    """Matches intercompany pairs."""
    global WS_IC_IDXC15, WS_IC_COUNTC15
    WS_IC_IDXC15 = 1
    while WS_IC_IDXC15 <= WS_IC_COUNTC15:
        perform_37325_find_ic_counterpart_C15()
        WS_IC_IDXC15 += 1

def perform_37325_find_ic_counterpart_C15() -> None:
    """Finds intercompany counterpart."""
    global WS_IC_IDXC15, WS_SEARCH_FROMC15, WS_SEARCH_TOC15, IC_FROM_ENTITYC15, IC_TO_ENTITYC15, WS_IC_IDX2C15, WS_IC_COUNTC15, IC_AMOUNTC15, WS_IC_DIFFC15
    WS_SEARCH_FROMC15 = IC_FROM_ENTITYC15[WS_IC_IDXC15 - 1]
    WS_SEARCH_TOC15 = IC_TO_ENTITYC15[WS_IC_IDXC15 - 1]
    WS_IC_IDX2C15 = 1
    while WS_IC_IDX2C15 <= WS_IC_COUNTC15:
        if IC_FROM_ENTITYC15[WS_IC_IDX2C15 - 1] == WS_SEARCH_TOC15:
            if IC_TO_ENTITYC15[WS_IC_IDX2C15 - 1] == WS_SEARCH_FROMC15:
                WS_IC_DIFFC15 = IC_AMOUNTC15[WS_IC_IDXC15 - 1] + IC_AMOUNTC15[WS_IC_IDX2C15 - 1]
                if WS_IC_DIFFC15 != Decimal(0):
                    perform_37326_log_ic_diff_C15()
                break
        WS_IC_IDX2C15 += 1

def perform_37326_log_ic_diff_C15() -> None:
    """Logs intercompany difference."""
    global WS_IC_DIFF_RECC15, WS_SEARCH_FROMC15, WS_SEARCH_TOC15, WS_IC_DIFFC15, IC_DIFF_RECORDC15
    WS_IC_DIFF_RECC15 = WsIcDiffRecC15()
    WS_IC_DIFF_RECC15.icd_from  = None  # TODO: was WS_SEARCH_FROMC15
    WS_IC_DIFF_RECC15.icd_to  = None  # TODO: was WS_SEARCH_TOC15
    WS_IC_DIFF_RECC15.icd_amount  = None  # TODO: was WS_IC_DIFFC15
    IC_DIFF_RECORDC15 = str(WS_IC_DIFF_RECC15)

    print('INTERCOMPANY RECONCILIATION COMPLETE')

def perform_38410_move_to_archive_C15() -> None:
    """Moves audit logs to archive."""

    while WS_EOF_FLAGC15 != 'Y':
        try:
            WS_AUDIT_RECORDC15  = None  # TODO: was AUDIT_FILEC15
            if WS_AUDIT_TIMESTAMPC15 < WS_ARCHIVE_DATEC15:
                ARCHIVE_AUDIT_RECORDC15  = None  # TODO: was WS_AUDIT_RECORDC15
                pass #Delete AUDIT_FILE
        except:
            WS_EOF_FLAGC15 = 'Y'
    WS_EOF_FLAGC15 = 'N'

def perform_39140_transaction_metrics_C15() -> None:
    """Collects transaction metrics."""
    global WS_TPSC15, WS_TRANS_COUNTC15, WS_ELAPSED_SECONDSC15, WS_AVG_RESPONSEC15, WS_TOTAL_RESPONSE_TIMEC15
    WS_TRANS_COUNTC15 = 100
    WS_ELAPSED_SECONDSC15 = 60
    WS_TOTAL_RESPONSE_TIMEC15 = 5000
    WS_TPSC15 = Decimal(WS_TRANS_COUNTC15) / Decimal(WS_ELAPSED_SECONDSC15)
    WS_AVG_RESPONSEC15 = Decimal(WS_TOTAL_RESPONSE_TIMEC15) / Decimal(WS_TRANS_COUNTC15)

def perform_39200_analyze_performance_C15() -> None:
    """Analyzes performance metrics."""
    global WS_AVG_RESPONSEC15, WS_RESPONSE_THRESHOLDC15, WS_PERF_DEGRADEDC15, WS_TPSC15, WS_MIN_TPS_THRESHOLDC15, WS_THROUGHPUT_LOWC15
    WS_RESPONSE_THRESHOLDC15 = 60
    WS_MIN_TPS_THRESHOLDC15 = 1
    if WS_AVG_RESPONSEC15 > WS_RESPONSE_THRESHOLDC15:
        WS_PERF_DEGRADEDC15 = 'Y'
    if WS_TPSC15 < WS_MIN_TPS_THRESHOLDC15:
        WS_THROUGHPUT_LOWC15 = 'Y'

def perform_39300_generate_alerts_C15() -> None:
    """Generates performance alerts."""
    global WS_CPU_ALERTC15, WS_MEMORY_ALERTC15, WS_PERF_DEGRADEDC15
    if WS_CPU_ALERTC15 == 'Y':
        perform_39310_send_cpu_alert_C15()
    if WS_MEMORY_ALERTC15 == 'Y':
        perform_39320_send_memory_alert_C15()
    if WS_PERF_DEGRADEDC15 == 'Y':
        perform_39330_send_perf_alert_C15()
